With this alignment, LA-SR effectively super-resolves real LR inputs, producing realistic outputs that overcome the limitations of synthetic-data-trained methods.

</details>


### [72] [Domain Adaptive Object Detection via Dual-Stream Bilevel-Cycle Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31373)
*Yannan Chen,Wenqiang Wang,Ruoyu Chen,Jiancheng Wang,Mingbo Yang,Yaowei Wang,Wei Wang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对Cycle Self-Training (CST)从图像分类扩展到目标检测时面临的三大挑战（不可靠伪标签、训练不稳定、回归损失爆炸），提出了Dual-Stream Bilevel-Cycle Optimization框架，通过在Mean Teacher上构建CST、额外归一化处理回归边界框及理论推导回归边界，在四个跨域标准场景中取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cycle Self-Training (CST)在图像分类中有效，但直接扩展到目标检测面临三个挑战：1) 三类不可靠伪标签（分类错误、定位错误、两者组合）限制性能上界；2) 多目标检测导致训练不稳定；3) 回归坐标数值范围宽导致损失爆炸。

Method: 提出Dual-Stream Bilevel-Cycle Optimization框架：1) 将CST同时应用于分类和回归分支；2) 在Mean Teacher上构建CST以防止训练不稳定；3) 使用额外归一化将回归边界框映射到标准化空间，解决损失爆炸问题；4) 提供回归边界的理论推导。

Result: 在四个跨域标准场景（如合成到真实、天气变化等）上进行大量实验，结果表明所提框架取得了显著且可观的结果。

Conclusion: 通过双流双层循环优化框架有效解决了CST从图像分类扩展到目标检测中的伪标签不可靠、训练不稳定和回归损失爆炸问题，在无监督域适应目标检测任务上表现出色。

Abstract: Cycle self-training (CST) breaks the shared classifier assumption of the standard self-training framework, which is effective for unsupervised domain adaptation and exploits unlabeled target data by training with target pseudo-labels. CST introduces a target classifier and employs an inner-outer loop updating strategy, addressing the issue of unreliable pseudo-labels and enabling pseudo-labels to generalize across domains. Despite its success in image classification, extending CST to object detection faces three main challenges. First, the upper bound of CST in object detection is constrained by three types of unreliable pseudo-labels, such as classification error alone, localization error alone, and their combination. Second, since object detection involves detecting multiple target objects, directly applying CST leads to training insta bility. Third, a wider numerical range of regression coordinates leads to exploding losses. To this end, we apply CST to both classification and regression and propose the Dual-Stream Bilevel-Cycle Optimization framework. Specifically, we construct CST upon Mean Teacher to prevent training instability and use extra normalization to map the regression bounding box into a standardized space, effectively addressing exploding losses. Also, we provide a theoretical derivation of the regression bound. Extensive experiments across four cross domain standard scenarios demonstrate that our framework achieves considerable results.

</details>


### [73] [MAPE: Defending Against Transferable Adversarial Attacks Using Multi-Source Adversarial Perturbations Elimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31378)
*Xinlei Liu,Jichao Xie,Tao Hu,Peng Yi,Yuxiang Hu,Shumin Huo,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多源对抗扰动消除（MAPE）防御方法，包含单源对抗扰动消除（SAPE）机制和预训练模型概率调度算法（PPSA），在CIFAR-10和Mini-ImageNet数据集上分别达到95.1%和71.5%以上的平均防御率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络易受精心制作的对抗样本攻击，其中可迁移性对抗攻击因与正常输入模式一致且不依赖目标模型信息，具有极高的隐蔽性和检测难度，成为防御研究的重点。

Method: MAPE方法包含两个核心组件：1）SAPE机制，采用精心设计的通道注意力U-Net作为防御模型，利用预训练模型生成的对抗样本进行训练，消除已知对抗扰动；2）PPSA算法，引入模型差异量化和负动量，策略性地调度多个预训练模型，最大化训练时对抗样本间的差异，增强防御模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 在ResNet-34作为目标模型的黑盒攻击场景下，MAPE在CIFAR-10上平均防御率超过95.1%，在Mini-ImageNet上超过71.5%，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: MAPE能够有效消除各类对抗样本中的对抗扰动，为来自不同替代模型的攻击提供鲁棒防御。

Abstract: Neural networks are vulnerable to meticulously crafted adversarial examples, leading to high-confidence misclassifications in image classification tasks. Due to their consistency with regular input patterns and the absence of reliance on the target model and its output information, transferable adversarial attacks exhibit a notably high stealthiness and detection difficulty, making them a significant focus of defense. In this work, we propose a deep learning defense known as multi-source adversarial perturbations elimination (MAPE) to counter diverse transferable attacks. MAPE comprises the single-source adversarial perturbation elimination (SAPE) mechanism and the pre-trained models probabilistic scheduling algorithm (PPSA). SAPE utilizes a thoughtfully designed channel-attention U-Net as the defense model and employs adversarial examples generated by a pre-trained model (e.g., ResNet) for its training, thereby enabling the elimination of known adversarial perturbations. PPSA introduces model difference quantification and negative momentum to strategically schedule multiple pre-trained models, thereby maximizing the differences among adversarial examples during the defense model's training and enhancing its robustness in eliminating adversarial perturbations. MAPE effectively eliminates adversarial perturbations in various adversarial examples, providing a robust defense against attacks from different substitute models. In a black-box attack scenario utilizing ResNet-34 as the target model, our approach achieves average defense rates of over 95.1\% on CIFAR-10 and over 71.5\% on Mini-ImageNet, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [74] [MS-Resampler: Multi-Scope Visual Resampling for Efficient Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31383)
*Zhongyang Li,Yaqian Li,Faming Fang,Rinyoichi Takezoe,Zi-Hao Bo,Cheng Qian,Mo Guang,Guixu Zhang,Kaiwen Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MS-Resampler，通过多范围视觉重采样框架，将显式空间范围先验注入重采样注意力中，实现从局部到全局的多粒度视觉信息聚合，在十个多模态基准上优于传统单范围重采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM中的重采样器采用单一固定的全局交叉注意力聚合范围，导致细粒度局部证据模糊，难以在固定token预算内同时捕捉局部细节和全局上下文。

Method: MS-Resampler实例化多个范围特定的重采样器，通过注入显式空间范围先验到重采样注意力中，每个分支以特定粒度（从局部到全局）聚合视觉信息，再自适应融合输出最终视觉表示。

Result: 在十个公开多模态基准上，MS-Resampler一致提升了视觉理解和多模态推理能力，且仅引入极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: 多范围视觉重采样框架能有效克服单范围重采样器的局限性，以低成本提升MLLM的多模态理解性能。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically employ resampling-based projectors to transform dense visual features into a compact token sequence for language modeling. Most existing resamplers adopt a single, fixed aggregation scope via global cross-attention, which can blur fine-grained local evidence and limit the ability to capture both local details and global context within a fixed token budget. In this work, we propose MS-Resampler, a multi-scope visual resampling framework for MLLMs. MS-Resampler instantiates multiple scope-specific resamplers by injecting explicit spatial scope priors into the resampling attention, enabling each branch to aggregate visual information at a particular granularity from local to global. The outputs of these scope-specific resamplers are then adaptively fused to produce the final visual representations for language modeling. Extensive experiments on ten public multimodal benchmarks show that MS-Resampler consistently improves visual understanding and multimodal reasoning over conventional single-scope resamplers, while introducing only minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [75] [One Video, One World: Turning Monocular Video into Physical 4D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31388)
*Junhao Chen,Boran Zhang,Mingjin Chen,Henghaofan Zhang,Saining Zhang,Congcong Zhu,Hao Zhao,Ruqi Huang,Zhihao Li,Yufei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVOW是一个无需训练的4D网格场景重建系统，能从单目视频重建实例级、可直接用于物理仿真的水密网格场景，在合成基准上实现了最优的布局和几何精度，速度比基线快1-2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D重建方法在渲染质量上表现优异，但其输出（隐式场、高斯原语、点云）缺乏物理仿真器和具身AI所需的水密拓扑、实例分离和标准化物理接口，无法直接用于下游物理仿真和编辑任务。

Method: 提出四阶段流水线：(1) 视觉-语言模型发现、标注并分类所有实例的运动类型；(2) 类别感知重建，对刚体生成逐实例网格，对可变形体生成拓扑一致的网格序列；(3) 迭代的渲染-匹配-优化过程恢复公制尺度和6-DoF位姿轨迹；(4) 基于物理的组装确保地面接触和物体间支撑关系。所有运动通过直接顶点变形建模，无需类别先验或骨架蒙皮。

Result: 在两个合成基准（静态和4D）上，OVOW在所有基线中取得了最佳的整体布局和几何精度，最低的光度和语义误差；单目视频推理速度比基线快1-2个数量级；下游物理仿真验证了其物理稳定性。

Conclusion: OVOW首次实现了从单目视频到实例级、仿真就绪的4D网格场景的无训练重建，填补了高质量渲染与物理仿真之间的鸿沟，并建立了首个结构化视频到4D评估基准，可同时作为合成配对视频到4D仿真数据的可扩展引擎，服务于未来4D世界模型和具身AI。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{OVOW}, the first training-free system that reconstructs \emph{instance-level, simulation-ready} 4D mesh scenes from a single monocular video. Recent 4D reconstruction achieves impressive rendering quality, but its outputs (\eg, implicit fields, Gaussian primitives, or point clouds) lack the watertight topology, instance separation, and standardized physical interfaces required by physics simulators and embodied AI. OVOW closes this gap with a four-stage pipeline: a vision-language model discovers, labels, and motion-classifies all instances; category-aware reconstruction yields per-instance meshes for rigid objects and topology-consistent mesh sequences for deformable ones; an iterative render-match-optimize procedure recovers metric scale and 6-DoF pose trajectories; and physics-grounded assembly enforces ground contact and inter-object support. Crucially, we model all motion, rigid and non-rigid, through direct vertex deformation without category-specific priors or skeleton rigging, producing watertight mesh scenes ready for downstream physics simulation and editing. We further establish the first benchmark for \emph{structured Video-to-4D} evaluation, with metrics for geometric correctness, instance separation, and physical plausibility beyond visual fidelity; the same pipeline doubles as a scalable engine for \emph{synthesizing} paired video-to-4D simulation data for future 4D world models and embodied AI. Across two synthetic benchmarks (static and 4D), OVOW attains the best overall layout and geometry accuracy and the lowest photometric and semantic error among all baselines, and on monocular video runs one to two orders of magnitude faster than the baselines, while downstream physics simulation confirms its physical stability.

</details>


### [76] [Temporal Preservation over Processing: Diagnosing and Designing Spatiotemporal Single-Stage Video Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31421)
*Karam Tomotaki-Dawoud,Anna Hilsmann,Peter Eisert,Sebastian Bosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TemporalLens诊断框架，用于检测视频目标检测器是否真正利用时序信息；同时提出YOLO-3D架构，揭示保持骨干网络时序深度是性能的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单阶段视频目标检测器在标准指标（如mAP）下表现良好，但无法区分模型是真正利用了时序上下文还是仅依赖单一信息帧，这一差距被掩盖。

Method: 提出TemporalLens诊断框架，通过受控扰动、结构化遮挡、时序打乱、冗余注入和分辨率退化来探测时序依赖；同时提出YOLO-3D模块化实时时空检测器，基于YOLOv8构建。

Result: 堆叠帧2D检测器在目标帧被移除时性能崩溃，而时空模型能从早期帧恢复预测；在YOLO-3D中，保持骨干网络时序深度在32帧时带来+3.7 pp mAP@50的提升。

Conclusion: 将"检测器是否真正利用时序信息"转化为可测量、可操作的问题，诊断框架与架构设计共同为时序推理提供了评估与改进工具。

Abstract: Single-stage video object detectors are increasingly deployed in time-critical applications, yet it remains unclear whether these models genuinely reason over temporal context or merely exploit a single informative frame-a gap hidden by standard metrics, which reward correct predictions regardless of how they are reached. We address this from two complementary directions: first, we propose TemporalLens, a model-agnostic diagnostic framework probing temporal dependence through controlled perturbations, structured occlusions, temporal shuffling, redundancy injection, and resolution degradation, revealing whether a detector actually uses information across time. Applied to stacked-frame 2D detectors and our YOLO-3D architecture, it exposes behavioural differences invisible to mAP: stacked 2D models collapse when the target frame is removed, while spatiotemporal models recover predictions from earlier frames, a signature of real temporal reliance. Second, we detail YOLO-3D, a modular real-time spatiotemporal detector built on YOLOv8, and show that simply preserving temporal depth through the backbone is the dominant performance driver (+3.7 pp mAP@50 at 32 frames averaged across scales). Together, the diagnostics and architecture turn "does this detector reason over time?" into a measurable, actionable question.

</details>


### [77] [No Prompt, No Leaks: A Robust Generative Steganography Framework via Prompt-Free Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31427)
*Jingwen Cai,Fen Xiao,Shuhua Deng,Xieping Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无文本提示的扩散图像隐写框架，通过级联仿射耦合模块建立秘密图像与隐层表示的确定双射映射，并融合风格语义先验控制生成过程，同时引入预测-校正机制优化逆向轨迹，在安全性、重建精度和可控性方面达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本提示的潜在扩散模型隐写方法受限于文本提示的灵活性不足，难以生成高质量隐写图像并准确恢复秘密图像。

Method: 提出无提示扩散图像隐写框架：1）级联仿射耦合模块（CACM）实现秘密图像与隐层表示的双射映射；2）将风格语义融入扩散过程控制隐层表示，保证视觉不可感知性；3）引入预测-校正机制，通过当前和预测下一状态的反馈迭代优化生成轨迹，缓解无条件逆向过程导致的轨迹偏差。

Result: 在安全性、秘密图像重建准确性和可控性方面，该方法与当前最优方法相比具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 风格语义先验与预测-校正机制的引入有效提升了隐写图像质量与秘密信息恢复的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Generative image steganography synthesizes stego images directly from secret information to achieve inherent security advantages. Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have recently emerged as a fundamental image steganography framework that modulates secret latent representations with text prompts. Limited by the inflexibility of text prompts, these methods still struggle to generate high-quality stego images and accurately recover secret images. In this work, we propose a prompt-free diffusion image steganography framework that integrates style semantic priors to control more robust and reliable stego image generation. Specifically, a Cascaded Affine Coupling Module (CACM) establishes a bijective, deterministic mapping between a secret image and its latent representation. Then, style semantics are integrated into the diffusion process to control latent representation and ensure visual imperceptibility in the generated stego images. To mitigate trajectory deviations stemming from the unconditioned reverse process, a predictor-corrector mechanism is introduced to iteratively refine the generation trajectory via feedback from the current and predicted next states. Extensive experimental results show that the proposed method achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods in terms of security, secret image reconstruction accuracy and controllability.

</details>


### [78] [Temporal Training Strategies for Left Atrium and Left Atrial Appendage Segmentation in Dynamic Contrast 4DCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31444)
*David Montalvo-García,Lauren Severance,Elliot R. McVeigh,María J. Ledesma-Carbayo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态心脏CT中左心房及左心耳分割的训练集设计策略，发现使用全部27帧训练在早期低对比度阶段表现最佳，但生理学选择的子集在充盈期后性能相当。基于前景的归一化可改善低对比度帧性能，但无法完全弥补差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态增强心脏CT可分析左心房及左心耳对比剂填充与廓清的时间模式，有助于评估房颤患者血液淤滞。但准确分割面临帧间对比度剧烈变化及仅有单帧标注的挑战，需在训练鲁棒性与标注噪声间权衡。

Method: 以nnUNet为分割框架，比较三种训练集设计：1) 最小两帧数据集（模拟临床实践）；2) 生理学选择的帧子集；3) 完整27帧序列。同时评估基于前景的归一化对分割性能的影响。

Result: 全帧训练在早期低对比度阶段表现最优；生理学子集从充盈期起性能与全帧相当；基于前景的归一化可提升低对比度帧的性能，但无法完全缩小与全帧训练的差距。

Conclusion: 训练数据的时间多样性对动态CT鲁棒分割至关重要，但精心选择的帧子集可在性能与效率间提供有效权衡，适用于下游应用。

Abstract: Dynamic contrast-enhanced cardiac CT enables time-resolved analysis of contrast filling and washout in the left atrium (LA) and left atrial appendage (LAA), with potential applications for assessing blood stasis in atrial fibrillation (AF). Accurate segmentation across all frames is required for such analysis but is challenging due to large temporal contrast variations and the use of a single annotation per registered sequence. This creates a trade-off between training for robustness and limiting label noise. In this study, we investigate how temporal training-set design affects nnUNet-based segmentation of the LA and LAA in dynamic 4DCT. We compare training using a minimal two-frame dataset reflecting standard clinical practice, a physiologically selected subset of frames, and the full 27-frame sequence. We further evaluate the impact of foreground-based normalization. Training with all frames yielded the best performance in early low-contrast phases. However, the physiologically selected subset achieved comparable performance from the filling phase onward. Applying normalization parameters derived from the full dataset improved performance of reduced datasets in low-contrast frames, but did not fully close the gap. These findings highlight the importance of temporal diversity in training data for robust segmentation in dynamic CT, while indicating that carefully selected frame subsets may provide an effective trade-off between performance and efficiency for downstream applications.

</details>


### [79] [Towards a foundational model for recognising diastematic Gregorian notation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31454)
*Daniel Kurek,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对格里高利圣咏符号的光学识别，设计了一种基于S-GABC的通用编码，将四个不同编码的数据集统一转换，并训练了一个端到端基础模型，在四个数据集上均达到最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四个格里高利圣咏符号识别数据集采用不同编码格式，缺乏统一标准，阻碍了模型的泛化能力和比较基准的建立。

Method: 基于S-GABC方案设计通用编码，转换四个数据集至统一格式，训练共享的端到端基础模型。

Result: 所训练的基础模型在全部四个数据集上均取得了最优性能（state of the art）。

Conclusion: 统一编码方案结合端到端基础模型能有效解决跨数据集的格里高利圣咏符号识别问题。

Abstract: Optical recognition of Gregorian notation has recently been attempted with end-to-end methods, with four datasets introduced. However, each of these datasets is in a different encoding. We design a common encoding based on the S-GABC proposal, convert all four datasets to this common encoding, and train a shared end-to-end foundational model for diastematic Gregorian notation that establishes a new state of the art across all four datasets.

</details>


### [80] [AeroVerse-SatAgent: UAV-Satellite Collaborative Spatial Reasoning Inspired by the Dual Visual Pathway Theory of Cognitive Neuroscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31467)
*Wenyi Zhang,Fanglong Yao,Youzhi Liu,Peng Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Chen Gao,Xian Sun,Kun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatAgent是一种无人机-卫星协同空间推理模型，通过双通路视觉机制、几何感知3D重建编码器、多视图拓扑-语义对齐模块和一致性损失，在复杂城市环境中实现鲁棒的空间推理，性能超过现有最佳模型25.91%和11.69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机空间推理方法受限于单视角感知（易受遮挡和透视畸变影响），且大多数视觉语言模型缺乏显式几何建模，依赖语义线索导致视角和尺度变化下推理不一致。需要一种融合多视角几何信息的鲁棒空间推理方法。

Method: 1) 几何感知3D重建编码器：将2D无人机特征提升为显式3D空间表示；2) 多视图拓扑-语义对齐模块：在统一BEV坐标系下融合跨视图特征；3) 多视角一致性损失：鼓励视角不变表示；4) 构建SatAgent-SR130K大规模无人机-卫星协同多视图空间推理数据集。

Result: 在多种任务上，SatAgent以25.91%和11.69%的优势分别超越通用基础模型和专业空间推理模型，在复杂几何关系推理中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 无人机-卫星协同的多视角几何感知方法能有效提升空间推理的鲁棒性和准确性，验证了双通路视觉机制在航空具身智能中的有效性。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of aerospace embodied intelligence, enabling Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) to autonomously understand and reason about complex environments has become increasingly important. However, existing UAV-based spatial reasoning approaches face critical limitations: single-view perception renders them vulnerable to occlusions and perspective distortions, while most VLMs lack explicit geometric modeling, relying on semantic cues and yielding inconsistent reasoning under viewpoint and scale variations. To address these challenges, we propose SatAgent, a UAV-Satellite collaborative spatial reasoning model inspired by the dual-pathway mechanism of the human visual system. By jointly leveraging satellite and UAV perspectives, SatAgent enables robust, accurate reasoning in complex urban environments. We first introduce a Geometric-Aware 3D Reconstruction Encoder that elevates 2D UAV features into explicit 3D spatial representations. Next, we design a multi-view topology-semantic alignment module integrating cross-view features within a unified BEV coordinate system. We further introduce a multi-view consistency loss encouraging viewpoint-invariant representations. Finally, we construct SatAgent-SR130K, the first large-scale UAV-Satellite collaborative multi-view spatial reasoning dataset. Experiments show SatAgent outperforms state-of-the-art general-purpose foundation models and specialized spatial reasoning models by 25.91\% and 11.69\%, respectively, across diverse tasks, achieving particularly high accuracy in complex geometric relationship reasoning.

</details>


### [81] [Think While You Map: Asynchronous Vision-Language Agents for Incremental 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31471)
*Deniz Bickici,Michael Pabst,Shohei Mori,Dieter Schmalstieg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种异步架构的开放词汇3D场景图方法，将轻量级在线建图与重量级语义精化并行运行，在探索过程中即可查询场景图，并在多个基准上达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D场景图方法通常采用先重建再语义增强的两阶段顺序流程，导致场景图在探索过程中不可查询。作者认为这种顺序耦合是不必要的。

Method: 1) 异步架构：轻量级在线建图（基于概率体素的骨干网络增量维护稳定的物体身份）与重量级语义精化（后台VLM智能体逐步丰富场景图）并发运行。2) 通过语义闭环解决重复物体轨迹。3) 细粒度视觉属性附着和物体间空间关系推导。4) 多目标帧调度器，选择少量信息丰富的帧联合覆盖多个目标，摊销VLM计算成本。

Result: 在语义分割任务上匹配或超越现有方法（ScanNet, Replica）；在三个视觉定位基准（Sr3D+, Nr3D, ScanRefer）上以A@0.25指标超越先前最优方法15.3至18.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 异步解耦的架构使得场景图在探索过程中即可查询，且语义丰富度随时间逐步增长，同时通过帧调度有效控制计算成本。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene graph methods typically operate in two stages: first reconstruct, then enrich with vision-language models, leaving the graph unqueryable during exploration. We argue that this sequential coupling is unnecessary and propose an asynchronous architecture in which lightweight online mapping runs concurrently with heavyweight semantic refinement. A probabilistic voxel-based backbone maintains stable object identities incrementally, while background VLM agents progressively enrich the graph. This framework resolves duplicate object tracks through semantic loop closure, attaches fine-grained visual attributes and derives spatial relations between objects. A multi-target frame scheduler amortizes VLM cost by selecting a small set of informative frames that jointly cover multiple targets. The resulting scene graph is queryable during exploration and grows in semantic richness over time. Our method matches or outperforms existing open-vocabulary 3D scene graph methods on semantic segmentation (ScanNet, Replica) and surpasses the prior state-of-the-art across three visual grounding benchmarks (Sr3D+, Nr3D, ScanRefer) by 15.3 to 18.8 A@0.25. Project page: https://denizbickici.github.io/thinkgraphs/

</details>


### [82] [DrivingDepth: Sparse-Prompted Pixel-wise Scale Correction for Driving Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31488)
*Chi Huang,Wenhao Zhang,Hang Yin,YuAn Wang,Hao Li,Bosheng Wang,Xun Sun,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DrivingDepth提出将稀疏激光雷达作为几何提示，通过残差逐像素尺度校正对冻结的基础模型进行局部校准，在保留密集视觉几何结构的同时实现度量深度估计，在nuScenes上以4帧环视输入取得AbsRel 11.19和EdgeCR 5.741，优于MapAnything。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的密集深度估计面临几何-尺度冲突：深度基础模型能提供像素对齐的密集视觉几何但缺乏可靠度量尺度，而投影激光雷达虽提供度量锚点却稀疏、有噪声且与图像结构未对齐。现有方法在引入激光雷达时从头重新生成深度，覆盖了基础模型的连贯几何结构，在视觉连续表面上产生结构伪影。

Method: 核心洞察是基础模型已捕获几何连贯的相对深度，无需额外学习表面结构，只需逐像素尺度因子将相对几何映射到度量坐标。基于此，DrivingDepth将稀疏激光雷达视为几何提示，通过残差逐像素尺度校正对冻结的基础先验进行局部校准，从而天然保留密集视觉几何结构。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，使用4帧环视输入，DrivingDepth达到AbsRel 11.19和EdgeCR 5.741，在度量精度和几何一致性上均优于MapAnything（11.99/1.914）。

Conclusion: DrivingDepth通过将稀疏激光雷达作为几何提示而非覆盖基础模型的方式，同时实现了最先进的度量精度和几何一致性，验证了残差逐像素尺度校正方法在自动驾驶密集深度估计中的有效性。

Abstract: Dense depth estimation for autonomous driving faces a geometry-scale conflict: depth foundation models deliver pixel-aligned dense visual geometry without reliable metric scale, while projected LiDAR provides metric anchors that are sparse, noisy, and misaligned with image structures. Existing sparse-prompted methods incorporate LiDAR by regenerating depth from scratch, overriding the foundation model's coherent geometry and producing structural artifacts on visually continuous surfaces. Our key insight is that foundation models already capture geometrically coherent relative depth; no additional surface structure learning is required-only a per-pixel scale factor mapping relative geometry to metric coordinates. Based on this, we propose DrivingDepth, which treats sparse LiDAR as geometric prompts that locally calibrate a frozen foundation prior through residual pixel-wise scale correction, preserving dense visual geometry by construction. On nuScenes with 4-frame surround-view input, DrivingDepth achieves an AbsRel of 11.19 and an EdgeCR of 5.741, outperforming MapAnything (11.99/1.914) by simultaneously delivering SOTA metric accuracy and geometric consistency.

</details>


### [83] [HVPNet: A Bio-Inspired Network for General Salient and Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31496)
*Jiawei Xu,Qiangqiang Zhou,Zhouping Li,Yanjiao Shi,Yugen Yi,Jiacong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HVPNet是一种受人类视觉过程启发的轻量级通用计算架构，通过视网膜整合模块（RIM）和皮层解码器（CD）实现多模态显著目标检测与伪装目标检测，在22个数据集上取得优异的精度-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态SOD和COD方法依赖复杂的跨模态特征融合与解码结构，导致模型参数过大且检测性能因结构冗余而不理想。人类视觉过程却能高效完成检测，受此启发，作者希望探索能否通过借鉴人类视觉过程实现更简洁的建模策略。

Method: 提出HVPNet，包含两个核心模块：(1) 视网膜整合模块（RIM），以视网膜多层次信息整合为概念隐喻，通过层级特定的多阶段整合策略有效融合多模态特征；(2) 皮层解码器（CD），将解码过程分解为低层和高层视觉阶段，抽象人类视觉皮层的层次化处理过程。

Result: HVPNet可扩展至4种模态的7个任务，在22个数据集上建立了优异的精度-效率平衡，无需任何花哨设计即获得出色性能。

Conclusion: 受人类视觉过程启发的简洁建模策略能够实现准确且高效的多模态显著目标与伪装目标检测，验证了生物学启发在计算机视觉中的有效性。

Abstract: In recent years, most research on multimodal salient object detection (SOD) and camouflaged object detection (COD) typically aims to improve performance through complex cross-modal feature fusion and decoding structures. However, this approach leads to an excessively large model parameter scale and often fails to deliver satisfactory detection performance due to structural redundancy. In contrast, the human visual process is able to efficiently perform salient and camouflaged object identification without such complex structures. This contrast raises an important question: Can we draw conceptual inspiration from the human visual process to achieve a simpler modeling strategy, and still realize accurate and efficient object detection? To answer this question, we propose HVPNet, a simple yet general bio-inspired computational architecture. Drawing on the multi-layered information integration of the retina as a conceptual metaphor, we designed a Retinal Integration Module (RIM), which effectively integrates multimodal features through a level-specific multi-stage integration strategy. To fully exploit these features, we further design a cortical decoder (CD) that breaks down the decoding process into low- and high-level visual stages, abstracting the hierarchical processing in the human visual cortex. Benefiting from these designs, HVPNet can readily extend to seven tasks across four modalities. Without bells and whistles, it establishes an excellent accuracy-efficiency trade-off across 22 datasets spanning these seven tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/jiaweiXu1029/HVPNet.

</details>


### [84] [Fully Automated High-Precision Segmentation of Retinal Atrophy and Ellipsoid Zone Thickness in OCT: A Reliable Tool for Real-World GA Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31502)
*Wolf-Dieter Vogl,Hlynur Skulason,Oliver Leingang,Ursula Schmidt-Erfurth,Amir Sadeghipour,Ariadne Whitby*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的全自动框架，用于在OCT图像中对GA相关生物标志物（RPE丢失、EZ丢失、EZ变薄）进行像素级分割，在298个SD-OCT体积数据集上训练，并在外部数据集上验证，取得了高精度（Dice RPE丢失: 0.88, EZ丢失: 0.87, ICC > 0.98），为临床诊疗提供可靠工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年龄相关性黄斑变性继发的地图样萎缩需要精确监测相关结构性生物标志物以评估疾病阶段、进展和治疗反应，但目前缺乏全自动、高精度的分割方法。

Method: 使用三个专门的语义分割模型分别描绘RPE丢失、EZ边界（包括中断）和Bruch膜；在涵盖完整表型谱的298个SD-OCT体积上训练，并在独立外部数据集上验证；评估了重复性、读片者间可靠性、B扫描密度影响及按病灶大小分层的亚组性能。

Result: 分割精度高（Dice RPE丢失: 0.88, EZ丢失: 0.87, Pearson's r > 0.99）；总EZ厚度测量平均偏差为2.15 μm（亚像素级）；重现性优秀（ICC > 0.98）。

Conclusion: 该全自动框架能准确一致地量化光感受器退化和RPE丢失，为临床试验和常规眼科诊疗中的GA评估提供了高度可靠的工具。

Abstract: Geographic atrophy (GA) secondary to age-related macular degeneration (AMD) requires precise monitoring of relevant structural biomarkers to assess disease stage, progression, and treatment response. This paper presents a fully automated, deep learning-based framework for the high-precision, pixel-wise segmentation of key biomarkers in optical coherence tomography (OCT) imaging: retinal pigment epithelium (RPE) loss, ellipsoid zone (EZ) loss, and EZ thinning. The proposed pipeline uses three specialized semantic segmentation models to delineate RPE loss, EZ boundaries (including interruptions), and Bruch's membrane. To ensure robustness and generalizability, the models were developed on a diverse dataset of 298 SD-OCT volumes representing the full phenotypic spectrum of AMD (GA:222, intermediate AMD: 40, neovascular AMD: 17, healthy: 19) and validated on an independent external dataset (n=43). The comprehensive evaluation was further strengthened using additional datasets to assess repeatability, inter-reader reliability, the impact of B-scan density on measurement accuracy, and subgroup performance stratified by lesion size. Results demonstrated high segmentation accuracy (Dice RPE loss: 0.88, Dice EZ loss: 0.87, Pearson's r > 0.99). Total EZ thickness measurements exhibited a sub-pixel average deviation of 2.15 $μm$, and segmentation reliability was confirmed by a strong reproducibility score (ICC > 0.98). By accurately and consistently quantifying outer photoreceptor degeneration and RPE loss, this fully automated framework provides a highly reliable tool for GA assessment in both clinical trials and routine real-world ophthalmic care.

</details>


### [85] [SimpleSearch-VL: A Simple Recipe for Multimodal Agentic Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31504)
*Ming Dai,Zhihong Lu,Jinjie Gu,Jiedong Zhuang,Yefeng Liu,Wankou Yang,Jian Wang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimpleSearch-VL提出了一种高效、可靠且实用的多模态智能搜索框架，通过改进智能体自身的搜索与验证过程（而非扩展数据、工具或辅助组件），在仅使用少量训练数据的情况下显著提升性能，并达到与Gemini-3-Pro竞争的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能搜索方法过度依赖扩展数据规模、工具或辅助模型组件，导致效率低下、可靠性不足且实用性受限。作者旨在通过改进智能体自身的搜索-验证流程来突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 1) 因子化自适应展开(FAR)：通过构建信息量更丰富的训练组提升采样效率，同时利用冗余样本缓解长尾延迟并暴露难样本；2) 证据验证推理：采用思维链验证显式评估检索到的视觉和文本线索与原始上下文的相关性；3) 轻量级工具接口与网页自摘要机制，无需外部依赖。

Result: 仅用5K监督工具交织轨迹和2K强化学习数据，SimpleSearch-VL在Qwen3-VL基础上分别提升8B和30B-A3B变体15.8和16.0平均分；30B-A3B模型性能与智能体Gemini-3-Pro相当。

Conclusion: 通过改进智能体自身的学习与推理过程（而非扩大资源），可以在极少量数据下实现高效的、可靠且实用的多模态智能搜索，达到业界领先水平。

Abstract: We present SimpleSearch-VL, an efficient, reliable, and practical framework for multimodal agentic search. Its core idea is to improve the agent's own search-and-verification process rather than scaling data, tools, or auxiliary model components. For efficiency, Factorized Adaptive Rollout (FAR) improves sampling efficiency by forming more informative training groups while using redundant samples to mitigate long-tail latency and expose hard samples. For reliability, SimpleSearch-VL performs evidence-verified reasoning, explicitly using chain-of-thought verification to assess the relevance of retrieved visual and textual cues to the original context. For practicality, SimpleSearch-VL keeps a lightweight tool interface and performs webpage self-summary within the agent, requiring no additional external dependencies. With only 5K supervised tool-interleaved trajectories and 2K RL data, SimpleSearch-VL improves Qwen3-VL agentic baselines by 15.8 and 16.0 average points for the 8B and 30B-A3B variants, respectively. The SimpleSearch-VL-30B-A3B model further achieves performance competitive with agentic Gemini-3-Pro.

</details>


### [86] [PRISM: Latent Composition Consistency for Single-Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31513)
*Junseong Shin,Tae Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM利用预训练VAE潜空间的低相干性特性，将单幅图像反射移除重新表述为潜空间中的线性分离问题，结合流匹配和对比学习在六个基准上取得显著领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间中sRGB形成模型的非线性特性导致透射层和反射层纠缠，限制了现有方法的泛化能力。作者观察到预训练VAE潜空间中图像层之间的相干性显著低于像素空间，为分解提供了更有利的工作空间。

Method: (1) 将SIRR重新表述为潜空间中的线性分离问题，利用预训练FLUX主干学习流匹配速度场，单次前向传播恢复透射和反射；(2) 提出Latent Composition Consistency (LCC)策略，通过跨样本交换反射潜变量构建合成混合物并施加循环一致性损失；(3) 提出Layer Contrastive Separation (LCS)损失，通过patch级对比学习促进层间语义分离，无需显式反射真值。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，PRISM持续且显著优于现有最先进方法，并对真实场景图像展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 预训练VAE潜空间是图像分解的理想空间，基于此的线性分离范式为反射移除问题提供了更有效且泛化性更强的解决方案。

Abstract: Single-image reflection removal (SIRR) seeks to recover the transmission layer from a mixture corrupted by reflections -- a severely ill-posed problem. Existing methods operate in pixel space, where the nonlinear sRGB formation model entangles the two layers and limits generalization. We observe that pretrained VAE latent spaces exhibit substantially lower coherence between image layers compared to pixel space, providing a more favorable working space for decomposition. Building on this finding, we propose \textbf{PRISM} (Pretrained-latent Reflection Image Separation Model), which reinterprets SIRR as a latent linear separation problem. Under an approximate additive formulation in latent space, PRISM learns a flow matching velocity field on a pretrained FLUX backbone that recovers both transmission and reflection in a single forward pass. To enforce robust disentanglement, we introduce a Latent Composition Consistency (LCC) strategy that constructs synthetic mixtures by swapping reflection latents across samples and enforces consistent decomposition via a cycle loss. We further propose a Layer Contrastive Separation (LCS) loss that promotes semantic separation between layers through patch-level contrastive learning, without requiring explicit reflection targets. Experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that PRISM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods by significant margins, with strong generalization to in-the-wild images.

</details>


### [87] [MV-GEL: Language-Driven Multi-View Geometric Entity Localization on Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31533)
*Kartik Bali,Roland Aydin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MV-GEL框架，利用多视角选择和VLM推理分割，从自然语言查询中定位3D多边形网格上的精细几何实体（如面、边），在面级IoU提升1.7倍、边级F1提升4.5倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在图像域的指代分割已取得进展，但将其扩展到结构化3D几何定位面临重大挑战：3D物体外观对视角高度敏感，单一视角可能导致目标实体被遮挡或变形。需要一种能够从多视角中选择最优视角的方法，以实现可靠的几何实体定位。

Method: 提出MV-GEL框架：1) GELviews模块——基于语言提示的视角排序模块，根据查询实体在特定视角下的可观测性对视角进行排序；2) 选择最优视角后，使用VLM推理分割骨干网络生成掩码；3) 通过几何感知的射线投射将2D掩码提升到对应的3D网格上。框架完全与CAD无关，仅依赖3D网格。

Result: 相比基线方法，面级IoU提升1.7倍，边级F1提升4.5倍以上，显著优于CLIP基和随机视角采样方法，尤其对薄壁和视角敏感结构效果突出。

Conclusion: MV-GEL通过提示条件化的多视角选择策略，有效解决了3D几何实体定位中视角敏感性的挑战，实现了语言驱动的精细CAD实体定位，且无需CAD先验知识。

Abstract: Identifying and grounding precise geometric entities, such as edges, planar regions, and curved surfaces within 3D objects, is foundational to computer-aided design (CAD), robotic manipulation, and scientific simulation. Although modern Vision Language Models (VLMs) have advanced referring segmentation (RIS) in the image domain, extending such language-driven localization to structured 3D geometry is substantially harder. The 3D object appearance is highly sensitive to viewpoints; a single perspective may render a target entity clearly observable, while another may suffer from severe occlusion or foreshortening. In this work, we attempt to solve these challenges with MV-GEL (Multi-View Geometric Entity Localization), a framework for localizing fine-grained geometric entities on polygon meshes from natural language queries. Our key insight is that reliable CAD entity (i.e., faces, edges or solids) localization depends on selecting views that make the queried entity maximally interpretable. We introduce GELviews, a prompt-conditioned ranking module that prioritizes viewpoints based on language prompted observability of geometric CAD entities. Selected views are processed by a VLM-based reasoning segmentation backbone, and predicted masks are lifted to the corresponding meshes via geometry-aware ray casting. Our framework is completely CAD agnostic and relies only on 3D meshes. Experiments show up to a 1.7X improvement in face-level IoU and over 4.5X gains in edge-level F1 compared to vanilla baselines, substantially outperforming CLIP-based and random view sampling, particularly for thin and view-sensitive structures.The dataset, code and trained checkpoints are available at https://github.com/kbali1297/MV-GEL.

</details>


### [88] [DataEvolver: Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Data Construction for Text-Rich Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31537)
*Siyu Yan,Yizhen Gao,Yilin Wang,Dongxing Mao,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DataEvolver框架，将文本丰富图像数据构建视为反馈驱动的演化过程，通过收集-验证-批评-生成的多智能体循环利用被拒绝样本中的失败信号，显著提升文本图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本丰富图像数据构建采用静态抓取-过滤-冻结范式，丢弃了被拒绝样本中蕴含的有用失败信号（如OCR错误、语义不匹配），导致后续构建重复相同失败模式。

Method: 提出DataEvolver多智能体框架：Retriever收集候选样本，Verifier分配质量分数和拒绝原因，Critic汇总回合级反馈为语义反馈，Generator针对未覆盖区域进行目标合成，更新后的反馈记忆指导下一轮构建。

Result: 在0.75M规模PixArt-alpha上，DataEvolver在TextScenesHQ上提升OCR-F1 85.3%，在LongTextBench上提升35.3%，改进跨基准一致且可迁移至Show-o2。

Conclusion: 被拒绝样本能够为改进文本丰富图像数据构建提供可操作的反馈信号。

Abstract: Text-rich image generation is one of the most challenging settings in image generation, since models must simultaneously produce visually realistic images and render legible, semantically aligned, and layout-consistent text. Existing data pipelines usually follow a static crawl-filter-freeze paradigm. They collect candidate samples, filter them once, and freeze the accepted data for training. However, rejected samples are usually discarded, although they often contain useful failure signals such as OCR errors and semantic mismatches. As a result, later construction rounds may repeat the same failure modes. To address these limitations, we propose DataEvolver, a self-evolving multi-agent framework for text-rich image data construction. DataEvolver treats data construction as feedback-driven construction policy evolution. A Retriever collects candidate samples, a Verifier assigns quality scores and rejection causes, a Critic summarizes round-level feedback into semantic feedback, and a Generator completes under-covered regions through targeted synthesis. The updated feedback memory then guides the next construction round. Experiments on text-rich image generation benchmarks show that DataEvolver produces more useful training data than fixed-dataset baselines under matched data budgets. At the 0.75M scale on PixArt-alpha, DataEvolver improves OCR-F1 over the strongest baseline by 85.3 percent on TextScenesHQ and 35.3 percent on LongTextBench. The improvements are consistent across both evaluated benchmarks and also transfer to Show-o2, indicating that the benefit of DataEvolver is not tied to a single downstream generator. These results suggest that rejected samples can provide actionable feedback for improving text-rich image data construction.

</details>


### [89] [AugSplat: Radiance Field-Informed Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31556)
*Lorenzo Lazzaroni,Riccardo Bollati,Daniel Barath,Michael Niemeyer,Keisuke Tateno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AugSplat是一个简单框架，通过辐射场视角增强来改进稀疏视图下的高斯泼溅。先训练辐射场合成新视角图像，再将其作为辅助监督优化高斯泼溅。提出了Staged AugSplat（分阶段训练）和Dual AugSplat（联合训练）两种变体，在稀疏视图mip-NeRF 360场景上提升了重建质量，同时保持实时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图场景下，高斯泼溅方法对初始几何质量高度敏感，有限观测常导致点云重建不完整或有噪声。神经辐射场虽能鲁棒重建但推理慢，高斯泼溅实时但初始几何敏感，两者互补特性未被充分利用。

Method: 先基于稀疏输入视图训练辐射场，从邻近新视角合成额外图像扩大视图覆盖；再将这些合成视图作为辅助监督用于高斯泼溅优化。Staged AugSplat先用合成视图进行初始优化再切换到真实图像，Dual AugSplat则对真实和合成视图联合训练并采用衰减的合成损失权重。

Result: 在稀疏视图mip-NeRF 360场景上，AugSplat优于标准高斯泼溅。Staged AugSplat平均性能最强，Dual AugSplat性能接近且全程保持真实图像监督，两种变体均保持实时推理。

Conclusion: 利用辐射场合成视图作为辅助监督，是提升稀疏视图下高斯泼溅质量的有效策略，结合了神经辐射场的鲁棒重建能力与高斯泼溅的实时渲染优势。

Abstract: Generating high-quality novel views at real-time frame rates remains a central challenge in 3D vision, particularly in sparse-view scenarios. Neural radiance fields have demonstrated robust reconstruction from limited observations, but their reliance on volumetric rendering leads to high computational cost and slow inference. In contrast, Gaussian Splatting methods achieve real-time rendering through rasterization, but their optimization is highly sensitive to the quality of the initial geometry. This sensitivity becomes especially problematic in sparse-view settings, where limited observations often lead to incomplete or noisy point-cloud reconstructions. In this work, we present AugSplat, a simple framework for improving Gaussian Splatting in sparse-view regimes using radiance-field-based view augmentation. We first train a radiance field on the sparse input views and use it to synthesize additional images from nearby novel viewpoints, increasing the effective view-space coverage available for supervision. These synthetic views are then used as auxiliary supervision during Gaussian Splatting optimization. We study two variants: Staged AugSplat, which uses synthetic views for an initial optimization phase before switching to real images, and Dual AugSplat, which jointly trains on real and synthetic views with a decaying synthetic loss weight. Experiments on sparse-view mip-NeRF 360 scenes show that AugSplat improves reconstruction quality over standard Gaussian Splatting. Staged AugSplat achieves the strongest average performance, while Dual AugSplat provides a closely performing formulation that keeps real-image supervision active throughout training, and both variants preserve real-time rendering at inference.

</details>


### [90] [Mitigating Positional Leakage in 3D Masked Autoencoders for Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31570)
*Xu Yan,Huiqun Wang,Chen Wang,Lei Ren,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MPL-MAE，通过抑制3D掩码自编码器中位置信息的过度依赖，促进空间先验与语义特征的平衡交互，提升点云自监督学习的表征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D MAE框架中，解码器过度依赖位置信息（位置泄露），导致语义表示学习被削弱，特征质量欠佳。

Method: 提出MPL-MAE框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 重新校准的位置嵌入模块，抑制度量主导的坐标信号同时保留几何拓扑；(2) 门控位置接口模块，在重建过程中动态调控位置信息的注入。

Result: 在下游任务的广泛实验中持续取得有竞争力的性能，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过缓解位置过度依赖，实现了空间先验与语义特征的更平衡交互，获得鲁棒且信息丰富的点云表征。

Abstract: Masked autoencoding has emerged as a prominent paradigm for self-supervised learning on 3D point clouds, achieving competitive performance across downstream tasks. Unlike its 2D counterpart, 3D masked autoencoding directly reconstructs spatial coordinates, making it inherently susceptible to positional leakage. In this work, we identify that the decoder in existing 3D MAE frameworks tends to over-rely on positional information, which weakens semantic representation learning and leads to suboptimal feature quality. To address this issue, we propose MPL-MAE, a masked point learning framework that mitigates positional over-reliance while enhancing the utilization of encoder features. Specifically, we introduce a recalibrated positional embedding module that suppresses metric-dominant coordinate signals while preserving geometric topology, together with a gated positional interface module that dynamically regulates positional injection during reconstruction. These designs promote a more balanced interaction between spatial priors and semantic features, yielding robust and informative representations. Extensive experiments across downstream tasks demonstrate that MPL-MAE consistently achieves competitive performance, validating its effectiveness. Code is available at https://github.com/yanx57/MPL-MAE.

</details>


### [91] [Temperature Field Reconstruction of Tungsten Monoblock Divertor on EAST using Physics-aware Neural Operator Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31574)
*Zikang Yan,Xiao Wang,Qingquan Yang,Zhendong Yang,Gaoting Chen,Zehua Chen,Bo Jiang,Jin Tang,Guosheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理感知神经算子变压器（PNOT），用于聚变装置偏滤器温度场的实时高精度重建，通过图注意力、物理感知神经算子和Sobolev正则化损失实现物理一致性的快速预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元方法计算成本高，无法满足聚变装置偏滤器温度场实时重建与控制的需求，亟需一种快速且泛化能力强的替代方法。

Method: PNOT将边界热流关系建模为结构化图，利用图注意力显式捕捉空间物理依赖；设计物理感知神经算子模块，通过切片聚合具有相似物理条件的查询点来建模热扩散；采用梯度约束的Sobolev正则化损失增强函数值及其导数的物理一致性。

Result: 实验表明，引入物理约束在保持物理一致性的同时提高了预测精度。

Conclusion: PNOT为聚变装置偏滤器温度场提供了快速、精确且物理一致的实时重建方案，有望用于实时控制与设备寿命延长。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of the divertor temperature field is essential for preventing material melting and damage and for extending the service life of fusion devices. However, conventional numerical methods, such as the Finite Element Method (FEM), are computationally expensive and therefore unsuitable for real-time applications. Therefore, a fast and generalizable method is required for real-time reconstruction of the divertor temperature field and subsequent real-time control. To address the above issue, we propose a Physics-aware Neural Operator Transformer (PNOT) to characterize the spatiotemporal evolution of the divertor temperature field. It models boundary heat-flux relations as a structured graph and employs graph attention to explicitly capture spatial physical dependencies. Inspired by physics-aware attention, we further develop a physics-aware neural operator module to aggregate query points with similar physical conditions via slicing and model heat diffusion, while a gradient-constrained Sobolev regularization loss enforces consistency between function values and their derivatives. Experimental results show that these physical constraints improve prediction accuracy while preserving physical consistency. The source code of this paper will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenFusion

</details>


### [92] [Localized Conformal Prediction for Image Classification with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31577)
*Clément Fuchs,Tim Bary,Benoît Macq*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了局部化共形预测在视觉-语言模型图像分类中的应用，发现直接使用余弦相似度无法提升性能，并提出了一种非线性变换方法，在保持边际覆盖保证的同时显著降低了平均集大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全条件保证在共形预测中不可实现，现有方法通过根据测试样本与校准样本的相似性调整共形集来近似这一行为，但在图像分类中的应用尚未充分探索。

Method: 基于开源实现的局部化共形预测算法，在视觉-语言模型上对自然图像分类任务进行广泛基准测试；提出对余弦相似度进行简单的非线性变换。

Result: 直接使用余弦相似度无法优于非局部基线方法；提出的非线性变换保持了边际覆盖保证，并实现了统计显著的均值集大小缩减。

Conclusion: 非线性变换的余弦相似度能有效提升局部化共形预测在视觉-语言模型图像分类中的性能。

Abstract: Conformal predictions have attracted significant attention in the field of uncertainty quantification, mainly because of their strong marginal coverage guarantees. Full conditional guarantee is not an attainable goal, a well known fact in conformal predictions literature. As a result, several approaches have tried to approximate this behavior by adapting the conformal sets of test-time samples according to their similarity to calibration examples. Although the latter has gained traction and shown impressive performances for regression problems, its application to image classification remains under-explored. We conduct an extensive benchmarking on natural image classification tasks with vision-language models (VLMs), using our open source implementation of a recent localized conformal prediction algorithm. We show that straightforward usage of the cosine similarity between test-time and calibration visual features, an intuitive choice for VLMs, is not sufficient to improve over the non-local baselines. In response, we propose a simple non-linear transformation of the cosine similarities, which conserves marginal coverage guarantees and achieves statistically significant mean set sizes reduction. Code is available at https://github.com/cfuchs2023/lcp-vlm/.

</details>


### [93] [DPPE: Rethinking Camera-Based Positional Encoding for Scaling Multi-View Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31585)
*Shun Kenney,Teppei Suzuki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现基于相机参数的相对位置编码在视角合成任务扩展训练中会导致性能瓶颈，原因是旋转和平移信息在同一维度存储造成辨识不明确。为此提出解耦姿态位置编码（DPPE），将旋转和平移显式分离，实现稳定长时训练并提升外推泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于相机参数的位置编码在扩展视角合成模型训练时，后期性能停滞不前，存在训练可扩展性瓶颈，亟需探究原因并加以解决。

Method: 提出解耦姿态位置编码（DPPE），将位置编码中的旋转矩阵与平移向量显式解耦，分别存储于不同的特征维度，避免两者在值向量中间一维度共存导致的辨识不确定性问题。

Result: DPPE在NVS任务上实现稳定的长时训练，在扩展训练设置下性能持续提升，并在外推设置（如增加视角数量、zoom-in场景）中表现出优越的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 旋转与平移在位置编码中的耦合是训练可扩展性瓶颈的关键原因，DPPE通过解耦设计有效解决了该问题，提升了模型在3D视觉任务中的扩展能力和泛化能力。

Abstract: The remarkable scalability of Transformers has expanded their application to 3D computer vision, where camera-aware positional encoding is crucial for providing spatial cues in multi-view geometry. Recent advancements have established the practice of using camera parameters -- such as extrinsics or projection matrices -- as relative positional encoding into the query, key, and value vectors of the attention mechanism. However, when scaling up the training recipe of novel view synthesis (NVS) models with the camera-based positional encoding, we observe a significant issue: model performance stagnates in the late stages of training.
  In this paper, we investigate the cause of the performance bottleneck when scaling up and demonstrate that storing rotation and translation given by the positional encoding in the same dimensions of the value vector causes indeterminacy in their independent identification, hindering training scalability. To address this, we propose Decoupled Pose Positional Encoding (DPPE), a novel camera-based positional encoding that explicitly decouples rotation and translation. Extensive evaluations on NVS tasks demonstrate that DPPE enables stable long-term training even in scaled-up training setup. Furthermore, it exhibits superior generalization performance in extrapolation settings, such as handling an increased number of viewpoints and zoom-in scenarios.

</details>


### [94] [Token-Sparse Medical Multimodal Reasoning via Dual-Stream Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31599)
*Kaitao Chen,Weiqian Zhao,Jiamin Wu,Qihao Zheng,Shangquan Sun,Chunfeng Song,Xiaosong Wang,Mu Zhou,Mianxin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViToS框架，通过双流强化学习实现视觉令牌剪枝与医学多模态推理，将视觉令牌减少至原长的77%，同时在多个医学基准上实现性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在医学图像推理中表现不佳，因为医学图像视觉证据极其稀疏，而当前缺乏一个统一的强化学习框架来同时实现主动视觉令牌剪枝与医学多模态推理。

Method: 提出ViToS双流强化学习框架：一个策略模型包含两个任务分支，一个分支负责定位（grounding），另一个分支在视觉令牌剪枝后进行稀疏令牌推理；引入交叉反馈序列优化解决耦合策略学习问题，避免梯度冲突并促进共享策略模型收敛。

Result: 在7个医学基准上，视觉令牌减少至原序列长度的77%，Lingshu-7B上相对性能达108.27%，HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B上相对性能达104.16%。

Conclusion: ViToS实现了优越的性能和推理加速，为医学多模态推理建立了一个高效范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) combining reinforcement learning (RL) ignite remarkable progress in multimodal reasoning, yet still struggle with medical images, which typically exhibit extremely sparse visual evidence to inform clinical decision-making. We recognize that pruning visual tokens outside the grounding region greatly enhances medical reasoning. However, a united RL framework for active visual token pruning (VTP) and medical multimodal reasoning remains unestablished. Here, we propose a dual-stream RL framework, ViToS, to fulfill token pruning and question answering. ViToS trains one policy model with two task branches, where one focuses on grounding while the other conducts token-sparse reasoning after VTP. Furthermore, we solve the coupled policy learning problem by introducing the cross-feedback sequential optimization, avoiding gradient conflict and facilitating convergence of the shared policy model. Evaluated on seven medical benchmarks, our method reduces visual tokens to 77% of the original sequence length while achieving a 108.27% relative performance on Lingshu-7B and 104.16% relative performance on HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B. Overall, ViToS delivers superior performance and inference speedup, establishing an efficient paradigm for medical multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [95] [Preserve the Hard, Regenerate the Rest: Uncertainty-Guided Synthetic Training Data Augmentation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31603)
*Nikolai Röhrich,Julian Gleißner,Ahmed H. A. Ibrahim,Silvan Mertes,Tobias Huber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于不确定性的引导式合成上下文增强策略，通过保留原始像素标签有效性并仅对不确定区域进行补全，在不依赖外部模型的情况下高效提升语义分割性能，在Cityscapes、UAVID、BDD100K上取得显著mIoU提升，尤其对稀有和困难类别效果突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割模型在数据稀疏、稀有或视觉多样性高的区域（如密集区域、小物体）表现不佳。现有合成增强方法存在标签与生成像素错位的问题，而现有解决方案要么依赖外部模型，要么采用粗粒度启发式策略（如不加区分地增强所有前景或背景），浪费计算资源在无信息像素上。

Method: 基于基线分割器的预测熵识别不确定语义区域，仅对互补的视觉上下文进行补全（inpainting），严格保持原始标签有效性。微调时仅计算原始像素上的损失，排除补全区域，使模型专注于未修改的不确定区域并在新上下文中学习。

Result: 在Cityscapes、UAVID、BDD100K三个数据集上取得显著的mIoU提升，尤其在稀有和困难类别（如公交车、火车、航拍视角下的汽车）上增益最大。

Conclusion: 不确定性引导的上下文增强是一种高效的语义分割改进策略，无需外部模型即可有效提升复杂数据集上的分割性能。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation models struggle with data sparsity and rare or visually diverse regions, e.g., dense regions or small objects in aerial or autonomous mobility data. While synthetic augmentation is an appealing solution, directly generating new labeled data risks misalignment of labels and generated pixels. Existing solutions to this problem often rely on external models, or employ coarse heuristics such as indiscriminately augmenting all foreground objects or entire backgrounds, which wastes capacity on uninformative pixels. To address this, we propose an uncertainty-guided synthetic context augmentation strategy that strictly preserves label validity and efficiently maximizes pixel informativeness per synthetic sample - no external guardrails required. Using a baseline segmenter's predictive entropy, we identify uncertain semantic regions and inpaint only the complementary visual context. When fine-tuning the segmenter on this synthetic data, we compute the loss only over the original pixels, excluding inpainted regions. This focuses learning on the unmodified, uncertain regions while presenting them in novel contexts. We demonstrate substantial mIoU gains on Cityscapes, UAVID, and BDD100K with the largest gains on rare and difficult classes such as buses, trains, or (from the aerial perspective) cars. Our results demonstrate that uncertainty-guided context augmentation is a highly effective lever to improve segmentation performance on complex datasets, with code provided at https://github.com/XITASO/Preserve-the-Hard-Regenerate-the-Rest.

</details>


### [96] [Learning Structurally Consistent Representations for Multi-View Radar Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31609)
*Ali Zia,Muhammad Umer Ramzan,Abdelwahed Khamis,Usman Ali,Abdul Rehman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的高阶结构对齐框架，利用可学习的超图捕获雷达回波间的高阶依赖关系，并通过非平衡最优输运实现多视图雷达特征的无对应对齐，在CARRADA和RADIal基准上分别达到63.8%和83.4% mIoU，超越此前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达分割方法大多依赖基于网格的编码和成对交互，难以捕捉同一物理目标产生的多个雷达回波之间形成的高阶关系结构，且多视图雷达投影之间存在异构性和测量密度差异，缺乏有效的跨视图一致性建模。

Method: 1) 利用可学习超图精炼雷达特征表示，捕获空间相关回波间的高阶依赖；2) 采用非平衡最优输运（UOT）实现RA、RD、AD三视图特征的无需对应关系的对齐，适应不同测量密度和部分观测；3) 设计自适应注意力机制融合互补视图，在稀疏噪声条件下强调结构信息丰富的响应；4) 使用监督分割与跨视图一致性正则化联合训练。

Result: 在CARRADA上达到63.8% mIoU（+1.7），在RADIal上达到83.4% mIoU（+2.3），均超越此前最优方法。

Conclusion: 高阶关系建模对鲁棒雷达感知至关重要，所提统一对齐框架有效提升了多视图雷达分割的性能和结构一致性。

Abstract: Radar sensors provide reliable perception under adverse weather and lighting conditions, but their sparse, noisy, and weakly semantic measurements make dense semantic segmentation challenging. Most existing radar segmentation methods rely on grid-based encodings and pairwise interactions, which struggle to capture the higher-order relational structure formed by multiple radar returns from the same physical object. We introduce a unified higher-order structural alignment framework for multi-view radar segmentation. The proposed method refines radar feature representations using learnable hypergraphs to capture higher-order dependencies among spatially related responses. To ensure consistency across heterogeneous radar projections, we further align view-specific features using Unbalanced Optimal Transport (UOT), enabling correspondence-free alignment under varying measurement densities and partial observations. An adaptive attention mechanism then fuses complementary radar views while emphasising structurally informative responses under sparsity and noise. The resulting architecture learns structurally consistent representations across Range Angle (RA), Range Doppler (RD), and Angle Doppler (AD) views and is trained using supervised segmentation together with cross-view consistency regularisation. Experiments on the CARRADA and RADIal benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong radar-specific baselines, achieving 63.8% mIoU on CARRADA and 83.4% mIoU on RADIal, improving the previous best methods by +1.7 and +2.3 mIoU, respectively. These results highlight the importance of higher-order relational modelling for robust radar perception.

</details>


### [97] [What Memory Do GUI Agents Really Need? From Passive Records to Active Task-Driving States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31612)
*Chen Liu,Ling Chen,Hanzhang Zhou,Xu Zhang,Quyu Kong,Panrong Tong,Wenhao Wang,Xin Yu,Steven Hoi,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出主动任务驱动记忆(ATMem)机制，将GUI智能体记忆从被动存储转变为主动维护的执行状态，并引入STR-GRPO在线强化学习方法，使智能体学会根据任务完成贡献选择性使用记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆方法将记忆视为被动存储，智能体需要从累积记录中推断值当前的作用状态（是否已使用、待使用等），在长轨迹任务中因相似字段、重复值、干扰项和过期状态导致重复或遗漏操作。

Method: 提出ATMem主动维护执行状态，将每个值链接到其角色和当前状态；引入STR-GRPO在线强化学习方法，通过对比记忆开启/关闭的rollouts来估计何时使用记忆能改进执行，并加入记忆成本感知奖励以抑制无效记忆使用。

Result: 构建了具有挑战性的移动基准测试，要求智能体从大量近似条目中正确操作所有符合指令的条目并拒绝违规条目，评估长程执行中完成范围内工作且避免范围外操作的能力。

Conclusion: ATMem通过主动维护执行状态而非被动存储，结合STR-GRPO选择性使用记忆，能显著提升GUI智能体在长程任务中的可靠性，减少重复和遗漏操作。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents increasingly face long-horizon tasks that require reading, updating, and reusing task-relevant data across pages and applications. Existing memory methods treat memory largely as passive storage, where past observations are accumulated and retrieved when needed. Yet retrieving a value does not reveal its current role in the workflow. The agent must still infer from accumulated records whether the value should be used now, has already been used, or must wait for a later dependency. This implicit reconstruction becomes unreliable in long trajectories with similar fields, repeated values, distractors, and outdated states, causing repeated or missed operations. We propose Active Task Driving Memory (ATMem), which shifts GUI-agent memory from passive storage to an actively maintained execution state. ATMem maintains task-relevant information as a continually updated execution state that links each value to its role and current status, enabling action selection based on the current workflow state. We therefore introduce \textbf{STR-GRPO}, an online reinforcement learning method that learns to use ATMem selectively according to its contribution to task completion. STR-GRPO contrasts memory-on and memory-off rollouts to estimate when memory use improves execution, while memory-cost-aware reward discourages costly memory usage that does not improve execution. To evaluate whether agents can complete all in-scope work while avoiding out-of-scope actions over long-horizon execution, we build a challenging mobile benchmark. From a list of near identical entries, agents must act on every entry that satisfies the instruction and reject entries that violate its constraints.

</details>


### [98] [Robust Autonomous UAV Landing on Maritime Platforms via Multimodal Agentic AI and Active Wave Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31613)
*Francisco S. Neves,Pedro N. Pereira,Raul D. S. G. Campilho,Andry M. Pinto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦的多智能体协同降落框架，通过USV搭载3-RPU稳定平台与UAV结合，利用两个独立的DRL智能体（SAC用于甲板稳定，多模态RL用于无人机进近），在从平静到恶劣的海况下实现了100%的降落成功率，平均稳定效率达87.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中巡检海洋基础设施常受随机海况影响，存在高动能冲击、着陆后倾覆及传感器遮挡等风险，亟需一种能够在复杂海况下安全可靠地完成无人机舰上降落的解决方案。

Method: 采用解耦的多智能体框架，同步搭载3-RPU稳定平台的USV与UAV；利用两个独立DRL智能体——SAC智能体提供高频波浪补偿以稳定降落甲板，多模态RL智能体负责UAV的最终进近阶段。

Result: 在高保真海事仿真中，从平静到恶劣的15种海况下实现100%降落成功率；恶劣海况下平均稳定效率87.8%，96%的任务时长内着陆表面保持在水平面1度以内。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦多智能体框架通过波浪补偿与智能进近的协同，有效提升了无人机在复杂海况下的舰上降落安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial inspection of marine infrastructure is frequently compromised by stochastic sea states, introducing risks of high-kinetic impacts, post-landing toppling, and sensory occlusion. This paper proposes a decoupled, multi-vehicle landing framework synchronizing an Unmanned Surface Vehicle (USV) equipped with a 3-RPU stabilized platform with a robust Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV). The architecture utilizes two independent Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) agents: a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent providing high-frequency wave-motion compensation for the landing deck, and a multimodal RL agent for the UAVs final approach. Evaluated in high-fidelity maritime simulations, the system achieved a 100% landing success rate across 15 trials in wave states varying from calm to rough. Results show a mean stabilization efficacy of 87.8%, maintaining the landing surface within 1 degree of the horizontal plane for 96% of the mission duration in rough conditions, effectively contributing to safer landings.

</details>


### [99] [PrISM-IQA: Image Quality Assessment Made Practical for Smartphone Photography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31626)
*Shuyan Zhai,Jiaqi He,Weixia Zhang,Liang Wang,Zhenjie Lee,Zufeng Zhang,Kede Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PrISM-IQA将智能手机图像质量评估重构为多问题有序诊断任务，通过预测每个ISP相关问题的有序严重性等级（无、轻微、严重、关键），并结合累积有序编码与结构化推理，实现更符合实际ISP调优需求的细粒度质量诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IQA方法将感知质量简化为单一分数，与ISP调优实践中工程师需识别具体质量问题、评估严重性并判断是否可接受的真实需求严重脱节。

Method: 提出PrISM-IQA框架，将IQA建模为多问题有序诊断：1) 对每个ISP相关问题预测有序严重性等级；2) 采用累积有序编码保证等级间的单调性；3) 通过结构化推理捕获问题内的单调性及问题间的包含与排斥关系。

Result: 在重构的SPAQ基准（含53个ISP相关问题标注）及小规模专家标注真实数据集上验证了有效性，展示了问题级诊断能力、通过线性探测证明可迁移的感知质量表征，以及预测结果对ISP调优的 actionable 支持。

Conclusion: PrISM-IQA通过细粒度的多问题有序诊断范式，显著提升了IQA方法与实际ISP调优流程的契合度，为智能手机图像质量评估提供了更具可操作性和解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing smartphone image quality assessment (IQA) methods commonly reduce perceptual quality to a single score. However, this scalar formulation is poorly aligned with practical image signal processor (ISP) tuning, where engineers must identify specific quality issues, estimate their severities, and determine whether they are acceptable or require intervention. In this work, we introduce a Practical ISP-aware Structured Model for IQA (PrISM-IQA), which reformulates smartphone IQA as a multi-issue ordinal diagnosis problem. Rather than regressing a single quality score, PrISM-IQA predicts an \textit{ordered} severity level -- absent, minor, severe, or critical -- for each ISP-relevant issue, covering both global image-level artifacts and local content-dependent defects. To produce logically consistent predictions, PrISM-IQA combines cumulative ordinal encoding with structured inference that captures within-issue monotonicity as well as cross-issue subsumption and exclusion relations. We evaluate PrISM-IQA on a reconstructed SPAQ benchmark annotated with $53$ ISP-relevant quality issues and on a small-scale expert-annotated real-world dataset. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of PrISM-IQA for practical issue-level diagnosis, reveal transferable perceptual quality representations through linear probing, and further show how its predictions can support actionable and meaningful ISP tuning.

</details>


### [100] [LiteMatch: Lightweight Zero-Shot Stereo Matching via Cost Volume Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31636)
*Md Raqib Khan,Santosh Kumar Vipparthi,Subrahmanyam Murala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteMatch是一个轻量级立体匹配框架，通过创新的成本体积稳定化策略（CVC-Loss）和双编码器设计（CVCE+HFE），在不使用昂贵3D卷积的情况下实现了强大的零样本泛化能力，参数量仅3.36M-9.58M，在多个数据集上取得了竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的立体匹配方法虽然精度高，但依赖沉重的骨干网络和计算密集的3D成本体积处理，导致大量内存和运行时开销，且跨域泛化能力差，限制了实际部署。

Method: LiteMatch包含三个核心组件：(1) 双编码器——Cross-View Correspondence Encoder (CVCE)捕获全局跨视图交互，High-Frequency Encoder (HFE)通过FFT频域线索增强细粒度结构细节；(2) Cost Volume Consistency Loss (CVC-Loss)——针对softmax归一化成本分布的体素级二元交叉熵损失，鼓励尖锐单峰的视差概率分布，稳定成本体积并加速收敛；(3) 轻量级细化模块——以低迭代更新生成全分辨率视差图，避免繁重的循环细化。

Result: LiteMatch在Scene Flow、KITTI、Middlebury、ETH3D和DrivingStereo等多个数据集上实现了具有竞争力的EPE和D1性能，验证了轻量级架构在跨域泛化方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构可以在不牺牲精度的情况下实现跨域泛化，打破了以往高精度必须依赖重计算成本的认知。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in learning-based stereo matching, high accuracy is often achieved at the cost of heavy backbones and computationally intensive 3D cost volume processing, resulting in substantial memory and runtime overhead. More critically, these methods frequently struggle to generalize across domains, limiting their practical deployment. We present \textit{LiteMatch}, a lightweight stereo matching framework that achieves strong zero-shot generalization through cost volume stabilization-without expensive 3D convolutions. LiteMatch employs two complementary encoders: a Cross-View Correspondence Encoder (CVCE) to capture global cross-view interactions, and a High-Frequency Encoder (HFE) that enhances fine structural details via FFT-based frequency cues. To stabilize the cost volume, we introduce the \textit{Cost Volume Consistency Loss (CVC-Loss)}, a voxel-wise binary cross-entropy objective applied to softmax-normalized cost distributions. By encouraging sharp and unimodal disparity probabilities, CVC-Loss promotes stable cost distributions and enables rapid convergence. A lightweight refinement module further produces sharp full-resolution disparities with low-iteration updates, avoiding heavy recurrent refinement. With a flexible design ranging from 3.36M to 9.58M parameters, LiteMatch achieves exceptional zero-shot generalization, delivering competitive EPE and D1 performance across Scene Flow, KITTI, Middlebury, ETH3D, and DrivingStereo. Our results establish that lightweight architectures can indeed generalize across domains without sacrificing accuracy. \href{https://mdraqibkhan.github.io/Litematch}{\textcolor{blue}{Code}}

</details>


### [101] [Technical Report of RoboSpatial Challenge at CVPR 2026: Selective Reasoning Activation and Reference-Frame Disambiguation for Embodied Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31645)
*Yuxiang Xie,Qi Lv,Jianming Xing,Zijian Hong,Xiang Deng,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoboSpatialBrain 是一种基于 RoboBrain2.5-8B-NV 构建的视觉-语言模型空间推理增强方法，通过训练无关的推理时机制（强制<think>前缀激活、任务特定后提示、显式参考帧重定向管道）提升空间推理能力，在 RoboSpatial Challenge 中获得第一名，成功率达 80.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在通用感知方面表现强劲，但在具身任务所需的空间推理方面常存在困难。

Method: 1) 强制 <think> 前缀激活策略 + 任务特定后提示，引导模型在上下文和兼容性任务中进行刻意推理；2) 显式参考帧重定向管道，解决上下文任务中的相机中心与物体中心歧义；3) 探索在兼容性数据上微调 RoboBrain2.5 并分析其与提示的交互。

Result: 在 RoboSpatial Challenge 中获得第一名，在 RoboSpatial-Home 上总体成功率为 80.9%。

Conclusion: 训练无关的推理时机制能有效提升视觉-语言模型在具身空间推理任务上的表现，且与微调策略结合可进一步优化。

Abstract: Vision-language models achieve strong general perception but often struggle with the spatial reasoning required for embodied tasks. We present RoboSpatialBrain, our submission to the RoboSpatial Challenge at the Embodied Reasoning in Action Workshop, CVPR 2026, built on RoboBrain2.5-8B-NV. RoboSpatialBrain combines two training-free, inference-time mechanisms: a forced <think> prefix activation strategy paired with a task-specific post-prompt that elicits deliberate reasoning on context and compatibility tasks, and an explicit reference-frame redirection pipeline that resolves camera-centric and object-centric ambiguity for context tasks. We additionally explore fine-tuning RoboBrain2.5 on compatibility data and present a detailed analysis of its interaction with prompting. RoboSpatialBrain achieved first place in the RoboSpatial Challenge, with an overall success rate of 80.9\% on RoboSpatial-Home. Code is available at https://github.com/YuxiangXie2003/RoboSpatialBrain.

</details>


### [102] [Sparsity-Inducing Divergence Losses for Biometric Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31664)
*Dimitrios Koutsianos,Ladislav Mošner,Yannis Panagakis,Themos Stafylakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Margin，一种基于α散度的新型损失函数，通过将边界惩罚直接编码到参考测度（先验概率）中，替代传统对logits施加几何惩罚的方法。该方法在保留α散度稀疏特性的同时提升判别性嵌入质量，在IJB-B/IJB-C人脸验证和VoxCeleb说话人验证任务上取得竞争性或更优性能，尤其在低误接受率场景下表现突出，且极端的后验稀疏性支持高效内存训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于边界惩罚的softmax损失（如CosFace、ArcFace）在面部和说话人验证中表现优异，但新提出的α散度损失函数虽然能诱导稀疏解（α>1时），却无法自然融入标准的几何边界机制。作者希望桥接这一鸿沟，设计一种既能利用α散度稀疏优势，又能引入有效边界约束的损失函数。

Method: 提出Q-Margin损失，核心创新在于将边界惩罚从传统对logits施加几何惩罚的方式，改为直接编码到参考测度（先验概率）中。具体而言，在α散度框架下，通过修改参考分布来引入边界，从而在保持α散度诱导稀疏后验特性的同时，促进判别性嵌入的学习。

Result: 在IJB-B和IJB-C人脸验证基准上达到竞争性或更优性能；在VoxCeleb说话人验证上同样表现强劲。与在相同训练方案下的ArcFace和CosFace基线相比，Q-Margin在低误接受率（FAR）场景下持续改进，这对高安全应用至关重要。此外，Q-Margin后验的极端稀疏性实现了精确且内存高效的训练。

Conclusion: Q-Margin成功将边界机制引入α散度损失框架，在保持稀疏性的同时提升了判别性能，尤其在高安全要求的低FAR场景下优势明显，为大规模身份识别任务提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Performance in face and speaker verification is largely driven by margin-penalty softmax losses such as CosFace and ArcFace. Recently introduced $α$-divergence loss functions offer a compelling alternative, particularly due to their ability to induce sparse solutions (when $α>1$). However, standard geometric margins are designed for the softmax function and do not naturally extend to this generalized probabilistic framework. In this paper we propose Q-Margin, a novel $α$-divergence loss that introduces a principled probabilistic margin. Unlike conventional methods that apply geometric penalties to the logits (unnormalized log-likelihoods), Q-Margin encodes the margin penalty directly into the reference measure (prior probabilities). This formulation naturally encourages discriminative embeddings while preserving the beneficial sparsity properties of the $α$-divergence. We demonstrate that Q-Margin achieves competitive or superior performance on the challenging IJB-B and IJB-C face verification benchmarks and similarly strong results in speaker verification on VoxCeleb. Crucially, against ArcFace and CosFace baselines trained under an identical recipe, Q-Margin consistently improves at low False Acceptance Rates (FARs), a capability critical for practical high-security applications. Finally, the extreme sparsity of the Q-Margin posteriors enables exact and memory-efficient training, offering a scalable solution for datasets with millions of identities.

</details>


### [103] [SAMBA: A Scatter-Guided Masked Bidirectional Mamba Foundation Model for SAR Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31668)
*Ke Wang,Xiaoyi Pan,Zhaoyu Gu,Xiaofeng Ai,Zhiming Xu,Feng Zhao,Shunping Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMBA，一种基于散射引导的双向Mamba自监督预训练基础模型，用于SAR目标识别。通过线性复杂度Mamba编码器、散射引导的掩码策略（SG-MAE）和SpatialMix特征交互模块，在减少参数量的同时实现了SOTA性能，有效解决了SAR标注数据稀缺和Transformer计算复杂度过高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR自动目标识别（SAR ATR）在实际部署中面临标注训练数据稀缺的瓶颈。现有的自监督预训练方法中，Transformer架构存在二次计算复杂度过高的问题，而通用的掩码策略忽略了SAR图像特有的电磁散射物理特性，导致预训练任务与SAR成像机制不匹配。

Method: SAMBA框架包含三大核心创新：(i) 线性复杂度的Mamba编码器，引入序列中间类别token以降低计算瓶颈；(ii) 基于SAR物理先验的三层层次化散射引导掩码自编码器（SG-MAE）掩码策略，使预训练任务对齐SAR固有成像机理；(iii) 轻量级SpatialMix特征交互模块，增强跨区域特征融合。此外还设计了两阶段跨领域预训练流程来优化整体预训练过程。

Result: 在全部预训练配置下，SAMBA以明显少于CNN和Transformer基线的参数量持续取得更优性能。与标准MAE默认掩码策略相比，SG-MAE进一步提升了模型的少样本迁移能力。在覆盖分类和检测任务的7个下游数据集上，SAMBA在大多数指标上达到SOTA水平，充分验证了其跨多样化SAR解释任务的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: SAMBA通过结合Mamba的线性复杂度优势与SAR物理先验引导的掩码策略，为SAR目标识别提供了一种高效且通用的自监督预训练基础模型，有效缓解了标注数据稀缺问题，并展现出优异的泛化性能。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar automatic target recognition (SAR ATR) is critical for Earth observation and defense, but its practical deployment is constrained by scarce annotated training data. Self-supervised pre-training alleviates this label bottleneck, yet prevailing Transformer architectures incur prohibitive quadratic computational complexity, and conventional universal masking neglects the unique electromagnetic scattering properties intrinsic to SAR imagery. To address these limitations, we propose SAMBA (Scattering-Guided Bidirectional Mamba), an efficient self-supervised pre-training foundation model for SAR target interpretation. Our framework features three core innovations: (i) a linear-complexity Mamba encoder with a mid-sequence class token to mitigate computational bottlenecks; (ii) a three-level hierarchical Scattering-Guided Masked Autoencoder (SG-MAE) masking strategy guided by SAR physical priors, aligning the pretext task with SAR's intrinsic imaging mechanism; (iii) a lightweight SpatialMix feature interaction module to enhance cross-region feature fusion. We also design a two-stage cross-domain pre-training pipeline to optimize the overall pre-training process. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SAMBA consistently delivers superior performance across all pre-training configurations, with substantially fewer parameters than both CNN and Transformer baselines. Compared with the default masking strategy in standard MAE, the proposed SG-MAE strategy further boosts the model's few-shot transfer capability. Benchmarking on seven downstream datasets covering classification and detection tasks shows SAMBA achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on most metrics, fully validating its robust generalizability across diverse SAR interpretation tasks. Source code and pre-trained weights are publicly available at https://github.com/mynswkk/SAMBA.

</details>


### [104] [WorldRoamBench: An Open-World Benchmark for Long-Horizon Stability of Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31672)
*Ting-Bing Xu,Jiacheng Sui,Zhe Gao,Kewei Shi,Wenjin Yang,Zhicheng Liu,Zhaoxu Sun,Mingchao Sun,Hongyu Pan,Fan Jiang,Mu Xu,Qi Fan,Yong Li,Baoquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldRoamBench是一个面向交互式世界模型的长时程稳定性开放基准，从动作、视觉、物理、记忆四个维度评估模型，包含600+测试用例。评估10+模型发现无一能在所有维度上表现可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型基准仅在轨迹层面评估动作跟随，忽略了记忆和交互物理，无法全面诊断模型在长时程交互中的稳定性缺陷。

Method: 从四个维度设计创新评估指标：(i) 动作：逐帧动作度量，消除跨模型语义尺度差异，暴露轨迹层面隐藏的失败；(ii) 视觉：基于片段漂移度量，捕捉非单调的中序列崩溃；(iii) 物理：可控性门控评估，从力学、光学、3D一致性评判动作执行下的物理合理性；(iv) 记忆：动作解耦协议，通过过渡局部3D点云重建评估场景记忆，通过追踪+VLM推理评估主体记忆。包含Nature/Urban/Indoor场景、第一/三人称视角、WASD连续交互10-60秒。

Result: 评估了10+开源与闭源模型，发现没有任何模型能在所有维度上可靠地满足要求，最佳模型也仅达到中等分数。

Conclusion: 在WorldRoamBench上的进步是迈向稳定、物理可信、记忆忠实且可部署于真实应用的交互式世界模型的关键步骤。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in interactive world models (IWMs), existing benchmarks evaluate action following only at trajectory level and ignore memory and interaction physics. We introduce WorldRoamBench, an open-world benchmark for long-horizon stability across four dimensions, each with tailored innovations: (i) Action: per-frame action metric bypassing cross-model semantic scale disparity and exposing failures hidden by trajectory; (ii) Vision: segment-based drift metric capturing non-monotonic mid-sequence collapse missed by start-vs-end comparisons; (iii) Physics: controllability-gated evaluation over mechanics, optics, and 3D consistency, scoring plausibility under faithful action execution; (iv) Memory: action-decoupled protocol evaluating scene memory via transition-localized 3D point-cloud reconstruction and subject memory via tracking-plus-VLM reasoning. The benchmark comprises 600+ test cases across Nature, Urban, and Indoor scenes in first/third-person views with WASD 10-60s continuous interaction. Evaluating 10+ open/closed-source models reveals none reliably satisfies all dimensions; even the best achieves only moderate scores. Advances on WorldRoamBench are steps toward IWMs that are stable, physically grounded, memory-faithful, and deployable in real-world applications.

</details>


### [105] [REDI: Corpus Aware Patch Ranking for DINOv3 Token Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31676)
*Chanjong Im,Sebastian Diem,Thomas Mandl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REDI方法通过结合监督TF-IDF视觉词汇统计和注意力图，在DINOv3 ViT-B/16上仅保留46.8%的token（201→107），实现了84.706%的Top-1准确率，超越密集基线83.514%和纯注意力方法82.634%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉Transformer的token缩减方法（剪枝、合并、池化）缺乏明确的监督信号指导，作者希望探究在固定token预算下，如何基于监督参考合理分配各patch的重要性。

Method: 1) 将DINOv3最终块的patch表示量化为视觉词汇，构建类条件TF-IDF表；2) 对验证图像，用真实类别索引TF-IDF行，通过四个变换视图生成与中心裁剪对齐的TF-IDF图；3) 在同一裁剪上生成注意力图；4) 对两者分别进行min-max归一化后逐元素相乘得REDI分数；5) 根据分数排序执行keep/merge/compress操作。

Result: 序列长度从201降至107（缩减46.8%），REDI（基于注意力质量）在ImageNet-1K上达84.706% Top-1准确率，优于密集基线83.514%、纯注意力82.634%和纯TF-IDF 81.796%。

Conclusion: 类别特定的语料统计（TF-IDF）和图像特定的注意力提供了互补的patch排序信号，联合使用能有效提升token缩减后的分类性能。

Abstract: Most token reduction methods for Vision Transformers seek favorable tradeoffs between accuracy and efficiency by pruning, merging, or pooling patch tokens. REDI (Relevance for DINOv3 Token Reduction) studies this question through a controlled supervised reference: how should a fixed token budget be allocated across patches for image classification? REDI quantizes final block DINOv3 patch representations into a visual vocabulary and derives class conditioned corpus scores using supervised TF-IDF over visual words. For each validation image, the ground truth class selects a row of the TF-IDF table, and four transformed views produce a TF-IDF map aligned to a reference center crop. A separate dense pass on the same crop provides an attention map. After independent min max normalization, their elementwise product defines the REDI score. A fixed keep, merge, and compress operator then uses score rank to assign patch roles and score magnitude to weight merging and compression.
  With precomputed REDI scores, a frozen DINOv3 ViT-B/16 backbone, and the same linear classifier used for dense evaluation, the operator reduces the sequence length from 201 to 107 tokens, a 46.8% sequence reduction. The REDI variant based on incoming attention mass achieves 84.706% Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K, compared with 83.514% for the dense baseline, 82.634% for incoming attention mass alone, and 81.796% for supervised TF-IDF alone. The same corpus term also improves reduced classification for three alternative attention formulations relative to their attention only counterparts. Together, these controlled comparisons indicate that class specific corpus statistics and image specific attention provide complementary signals for patch ranking in this setting.

</details>


### [106] [ShellMaker: Language-Guided Exterior Completion under Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31680)
*Ruiqi Xu,Daniel Aliaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShellMaker 是一个语言引导的建筑外观补全框架，能在固定结构约束下为已有室内建筑生成风格一致的外墙和屋顶网格，结合参数化屋顶生成、LLM提示优化、材质检索和几何感知组装，适用于多种输入格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内场景生成方法只能生成结构外壳，缺乏风格一致的立面与屋顶，而约束条件（足迹、墙体、开孔语义）必须保持不变，现有无约束生成模型常违反这些限制。

Method: 结合参数化屋顶生成、LLM驱动的部件感知提示优化、联合墙-屋顶材质检索以及几何感知组装，基于格式无关的脚手架表示，在固定结构约束下生成完整外观网格和PBR材质。

Result: 在多种输入格式（室内生成器、CityGML、CAD）上保持结构一致性，在架构连贯性上优于检索基线和无约束生成基线方法。

Conclusion: ShellMaker 能在保持结构约束的前提下有效补全建筑外观，具有良好的泛化性和架构连贯性。

Abstract: Despite advances in indoor scene generation, synthesizing coherent building exteriors consistent with generated interiors remains largely unexplored. Existing methods can generate floor plans and wall layouts but typically stop at a structural shell, lacking stylistically consistent facades and roofs. Completing these exteriors is challenging because the footprint, wall geometry, and opening semantics must remain fixed-constraints that unconstrained generative models often violate. We introduce ShellMaker, a language-guided exterior completion framework that operates under these structural constraints. Given a building scaffold and a text style prompt, ShellMaker generates a complete exterior mesh with PBR materials by combining parametric roof generation, LLM-based part-aware prompt refinement, joint wall-roof material retrieval, and geometry-aware assembly. Operating on a format agnostic scaffold representation, ShellMaker generalizes to indoor generators, CityGML, and CAD inputs, while maintaining structural consistency and improving architectural coherence over retrieval and unconstrained generative baselines. The project page is available at https://ruiqixu37.github.io/ShellMaker_web/

</details>


### [107] [Histogram-constrained Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31683)
*Haoming Liu,Yuanhe Guo,Yijia Cao,Shenji Wan,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出直方图约束图像生成（HIG）框架，通过最优传输理论在扩散模型采样过程中精确施加用户指定的分布约束（如颜色直方图或潜在特征分布），填补了全局语义控制（如文本提示）与局部稠密控制（如ControlNet）之间的控制粒度空白，并展示了在约束生成与信息隐藏等任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型控制机制在控制粒度上存在两极分化：全局控制（如文本提示）缺乏局部精度，局部控制（如ControlNet）需要稠密条件信号。缺乏一种介于两者之间的、灵活且可解释的中粒度控制方案。

Method: 将用户指定的直方图约束建模为最优传输（OT）问题，在扩散采样过程中应用显式引导变换，使扩散轨迹与目标直方图对齐。

Result: 在颜色直方图约束生成、潜在特征分布约束生成以及直方图级编码的信息嵌入等多样应用中验证了HIG的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 分布控制作为一种灵活、可解释且与现有控制机制完全兼容的控制方案，丰富了可控图像生成的混合策略，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as a dominant paradigm in generative modeling, enabling high-fidelity sampling from complex data distributions. Despite impressive capabilities, controlling diffusion models to produce outputs aligned with user intent remains an open challenge, especially when balancing global coherence with local precision. Existing control mechanisms vary in the granularity of their conditioning signals. For example, textual prompts guide generation globally through high-level semantics, while ControlNet-like approaches secure precise local structure via dense conditions.
