In this work, we introduce Histogram-constrained Image Generation (HIG), a novel control mechanism that falls into the middle ground of control granularity. Our framework enforces user-specified distributional constraints (e.g., color histograms or latent token distributions) during the generation process with exact precision. We model such control as an optimal transport (OT) problem and apply explicit guidance transformations during sampling, thereby driving the diffusion trajectory to align with the desired histogram. We demonstrate the versatility of HIG across diverse applications, including constrained generation via color/latent histograms and high-capacity information embedding through histogram-level encoding. Our findings underscore the promise of distributional control, a flexible and interpretable control scheme that is fully compatible with existing control mechanisms, diversifying the hybrid strategies for controllable image generation. Our project page is available at: https://maps-research.github.io/hig/.

</details>


### [108] [Semantic Occupancy Prediction with Dual Range-Voxel Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31688)
*Sitao Chen,Zhuangwei Zhuang,Hui Luo,Lizhao Liu,Qingyao Wu,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单次扫描点云的双范围-体素表示方法（DRVR），通过融合范围视图的上下文信息和体素视图的几何信息进行3D语义占用预测，在nuScenes-Occupancy上相比多次扫描方法实现了5.4%的mIoU提升和2.1倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的3D语义占用预测方法依赖多次扫描点云堆叠来获取密集空间信息，但这引入了额外的计算负担和位姿变换噪声问题，限制了实际应用。本文旨在利用单次扫描点云实现高效且鲁棒的语义占用预测。

Method: 提出DRVR框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 范围视图编码器提取紧凑的场景上下文；2) 几何感知体素视图编码器分别提取多尺度体素视图特征并组合以增强几何占用预测；3) 范围-体素融合模块通过体素到范围和范围到体素的双向融合协同两种视图特征。

Result: 在nuScenes-Occupancy、SemanticKITTI和SemanticPOSS上取得优越性能。在nuScenes-Occupancy上，单次扫描的DRVR相比多次扫描方法mIoU提升5.4%，推理速度加快2.1倍。

Conclusion: DRVR通过单次扫描点云即可实现高效准确的3D语义占用预测，消除了对多次扫描的依赖及其带来的效率和鲁棒性问题，为自动驾驶场景感知提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D semantic occupancy prediction, which aims to provide accurate and comprehensive scene representation, is crucial for autonomous driving systems. As point clouds suffer from sparsity and incompleteness, leading to insufficient semantic learning and difficult occupancy perception, existing methods often stack multi-sweep point clouds to obtain dense spatial information. However, such a naive strategy also results in efficiency (e.g., additional computational burden) and robustness (e.g., pose transformation noise) concerns, which hinder their practical applications. In this work, we propose a Dual Range-Voxel Representation (DRVR) that leverages the range-view context and voxel-view geometry of single-sweep point clouds for 3D semantic occupancy prediction, eliminating the concerns associated with the multi-sweeps. Specifically, we use the range-view encoder to extract the compact context of the scene. To fully exploit the spatial information, we design a geometry-aware voxel-view encoder that extracts multi-scale voxel-view features separately and combines them for better geometric occupancy prediction. Moreover, we propose a range-voxel fusion module to cooperate range- and voxel-view features via voxel-to-range and range-to-voxel fusions. Extensive experiments on nuScenes-Occupancy, SemanticKITTI and SemanticPOSS show the superiority of our method. Especially on nuScenes-Occupancy, our single-sweep DRVR achieves 5.4% improvement in mIoU and 2.1x acceleration compared to the multi-sweep method.

</details>


### [109] [Intrinsically Stable Spiking Neural Networks: Overcoming the Performance Barrier in the Absence of Batch Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31695)
*Ruichen Ma,Xiaoyang Zhang,Jian Bai,Guanchao Qiao,Liwei Meng,Ning Ning,Yang Liu,Shaogang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IS-SNN架构，通过拓扑感知的权重标准化和修改残差连接消除批量归一化层，在保持硬件效率的同时实现与动态BN方法竞争的性能，ImageNet上达到68.05%准确率，并减少96.4%的FPGA资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度脉冲神经网络（SNN）的性能通常依赖批归一化（BN），但先进的动态BN变体引入了运行时乘法操作，削弱了SNN的硬件效率优势。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，在不使用归一化层的情况下恢复SNN的累加导向推理数据通路。

Method: 首先识别出无归一化SNN中性能严重下降的主要原因是灾难性发放率衰减。基于此发现，提出IS-SNN架构，通过拓扑感知的权重标准化和修改残差连接来强制信号稳态，消除激活归一化层。标准化操作在离线阶段折叠为静态权重，从而消除运行时的统计跟踪和乘法操作。

Result: IS-SNN在VGG、ResNet和Transformer模型上达到与计算昂贵的动态BN技术相当或更优的性能，在ImageNet上达到68.05%准确率；克服了先前无BN尝试的深度限制；FPGA神经元实现查找表资源消耗减少96.4%。

Conclusion: IS-SNN作为构建准确且硬件友好的深度神经形态系统的实用框架，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了硬件效率。

Abstract: The performance of deep spiking neural networks (SNNs) often relies on batch normalization (BN). However, the advanced dynamic BN variants used in state-of-the-art models introduce runtime multiplications, which weaken the hardware-efficiency motivation of SNNs. To address this tension, we identify catastrophic firing-rate decay as a primary cause of severe performance degradation in normalization-free SNNs. Guided by this insight, this work proposes the Intrinsically Stable SNN (IS-SNN) architecture, which removes activation-normalization layers by enforcing signal homeostasis through topology-aware weight standardization and modified residual connections. By folding the standardization operations into static weights offline, IS-SNN removes the runtime statistics tracking and multiplications introduced by activation normalization, restoring an accumulation-oriented inference datapath. Comprehensive experiments show that IS-SNN achieves performance competitive with or superior to computationally expensive dynamic BN techniques across VGG, ResNet, and Transformer-based models. Notably, it achieves a competitive accuracy of 68.05\% on ImageNet and overcomes the severe depth limitations of prior BN-free attempts. Together with a 96.4\% reduction in FPGA lookup table resource consumption for neuron implementations, these results support IS-SNN as a practical framework for building accurate and hardware-friendly deep neuromorphic systems.

</details>


### [110] [Look But Don't Touch with Sparse Autoencoders for Unlearning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31699)
*Enrico Cassano,Riccardo Renzulli,Rayyan Ahmed,Marco Grangetto,Stephan Alaniz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）虽能可靠检测扩散模型中的语义概念，但其潜空间直接干预常导致分布外激活和视觉伪影。本文提出基于SAE检测的补丁替换方法，在保持激活统计特性的同时实现更干净的物体擦除效果，揭示了概念检测与概念干预之间存在根本性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并系统评估稀疏自编码器（SAEs）能否作为扩散模型中可控干预点的假设，探究直接潜空间干预与基于检测的替换方法之间的效果差异。

Method: 1) 使用SAE激活作为语义检测器识别目标物体区域；2) 将目标区域补丁嵌入替换为不含该物体的补丁嵌入；3) 对比直接潜空间干预（latent steering）与检测后替换两种方法在物体擦除和引导任务上的效果。

Result: 基于SAE检测的补丁替换方法相比直接潜空间干预，能显著减少视觉伪影，产生更干净的擦除效果，更好地保持扩散模型的激活统计分布。

Conclusion: 单语义或稀疏特征并非天然适合作为扩散模型的控制旋钮；SAE是强大的可解释性分析工具，但在直接操作（如概念擦除、去学习）中存在重要局限性；概念检测与概念干预之间存在根本性差距。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have recently been proposed as interpretable tools for concept-level manipulation, under the assumption that isolated features can serve as controllable intervention points. In this work, we systematically evaluate this assumption in the context of object erasure and steering in diffusion models. We show that while SAEs reliably detect and localize semantic concepts within diffusion model activations, direct intervention in their latent space frequently induces out-of-distribution activations, resulting in severe visual artifacts. To disentangle detection from intervention, we use SAE activations purely as semantic detectors to identify image regions containing the target object, and replace those patch embeddings with the ones that do not contain it. This detection-based replacement preserves the diffusion model's activation statistics and produces significantly cleaner erasure results than latent steering. Our findings reveal a fundamental gap between concept detection and concept intervention in diffusion models: monosemantic or sparse features are not inherently suitable as control knobs for steering. These results position SAEs as powerful interpretability tools for analyzing generative models, but highlight important limitations when used for direct manipulation, such as unlearning.

</details>


### [111] [Phantom: A Unified Face-Swap Deepfake Protection Framework with Latent and Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31703)
*Jungkon Kim,Cheolseung Jung,Jong-Min Choi,Juseong Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Phantom是一个统一的换脸深度伪造保护框架，通过联合约束潜空间和空间域的扰动，在保持视觉质量的同时有效提升面部隐私保护成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换脸深度伪造技术对个人隐私构成严重威胁，现有对抗方法在换脸场景下存在目标引导模糊、扰动溢出无关区域以及身份-风格解耦抑制对抗信号等问题。

Method: 提出Phantom框架：（1）自适应合成身份偏移但属性保留的目标，用于引导身份感知的潜空间优化；（2）在语义相关的面部区域施加掩码扰动，实现空间域约束。

Result: 在逃避场景下，Phantom在UniFace、INSwapper和SimSwap上分别提升保护成功率27.8%、25.6%和16.6%；在冒充场景下，保护成功率提升达10.2%，同时提升了感知保真度。

Conclusion: 联合利用潜空间和空间域约束能有效实现鲁棒且连贯的面部隐私保护。

Abstract: Face-swapping deepfakes pose an escalating threat to personal privacy by enabling unauthorized identity manipulation. While adversarial approaches have demonstrated success against black-box face recognition (FR) models, their applicability to face-swapping scenarios remains underexplored. In particular, reliance on fixed or random targets yields ambiguous latent guidance, and the lack of explicit spatial constraints causes perturbations to spill into identity-irrelevant regions. These issues are further exacerbated by identity-style disentanglement, which suppresses adversarial signals during deepfake generation. In this paper, we present Phantom, a unified face-swap deepfake protection framework that jointly constrains perturbations in latent and spatial domains. Phantom adaptively synthesizes identity-shifted yet attribute-preserving targets to guide identity-aware latent optimization, and applies masked perturbations confined to semantically relevant facial regions. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art face-swapping deepfakes demonstrate that Phantom improves protection success rates in dodging scenarios by 27.8%, 25.6%, and 16.6% on UniFace, INSwapper, and SimSwap, respectively, while also enhancing visual quality. Furthermore, Phantom generalizes to impersonation scenario, yielding up to 10.2% higher protection while improving perceptual fidelity. These results underscore the effectiveness of jointly leveraging latent and spatial constraints for robust and coherent facial privacy protection.

</details>


### [112] [WIDER-FAIR: An Annotated Version of the WIDER-FACE Dataset for Fairness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31704)
*Maxime Moussi,Benoît Ronval,Siegfried Nijssen,Félicien Schiltz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了WIDER-FAIR数据集，在WIDER-FACE基础上为每张人脸标注了感知种族（亚洲人、黑人、印度人、白人）和性别，并通过YOLOv5实验发现黑人群体的检测性能显著较低，验证了人口统计注释数据集对理解和评估人脸检测模型偏差的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的人脸检测模型存在跨人口群体的性能差异（公平性问题），但缺乏带有敏感属性标注的人脸检测数据集，难以研究和缓解此类偏差。

Method: 在WIDER-FACE基准上构建WIDER-FAIR数据集，手动标注了16,256张图像中每个人脸的感知种族（亚洲人、黑人、印度人、白人）和性别；使用人脸嵌入、K近邻分类器和t-SNE可视化评估标注质量；基于YOLOv5进行消融实验分析各敏感特征对检测性能的影响。

Result: 标注一致性得到验证；实验发现黑人群体的检测性能显著较低，且从训练集中排除该群体比其他任何种族群体排除时导致的公平性差距更大。

Conclusion: 带有种族和性别标注的数据集对理解和评估人脸检测模型中的偏差具有重要价值，WIDER-FAIR为相关公平性研究提供了基础资源。

Abstract: The deployment of face detection models in real-world applications raises important fairness concerns, as these systems may showcase performance disparities across demographic groups. A key obstacle to studying and mitigating such biases is the lack of face detection datasets with sensitive feature annotations. To address this gap, we introduce WIDER-FAIR, a new dataset built on the widely used WIDER-FACE benchmark, manually annotated with the perceived ethnicity and sex of each face. The dataset contains 16,256 images annotated across four ethnic groups: Asian, Black, Indian, and White, and two sex categories. We assess the quality and coherence of the annotations using face embeddings, a K-Nearest Neighbors classifier, and a t-SNE visualization, all of which support the consistency of the labeling process. As a demonstration of the dataset's potential, we train a YOLOv5 model and perform ablation studies on each sensitive feature. Among other findings, our experiments show that detection performance is notably lower for faces of Black individuals, and that excluding this group from training increases fairness disparity more than excluding any other ethnic group. These observations illustrate the value of demographically annotated datasets for understanding and evaluating bias in face detection models.

</details>


### [113] [UniCoder: Unified Visual-to-Code Generation via Symbolic Rewards and Reference-Guided Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31732)
*Yaozhi Zheng,Yilei Jiang,Manyuan Zhang,Yuxuan Wan,Kaituo Feng,Tianshuo Peng,Bo Zhang,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniCoder提出统一强化学习框架，通过符号属性对齐和参考引导代码优化两种机制，解决视觉到代码生成中像素级对齐难题，8B模型超越所有开源基线并达到与闭源模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLLMs通过SFT无法实现视觉到代码生成所需的像素级精确对齐，而RL方法受限于奖励粗糙度（语义指标无法惩罚细粒度偏差）和探索停滞（稀疏代码搜索空间难以引导有效策略）。

Method: 提出UniCoder统一RL框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 符号属性对齐——利用轻量辅助LLM将生成代码解析为离散视觉属性（如十六进制颜色、坐标范围），实现密集元素级奖励计算；(2) 参考引导代码优化——动态将真实轨迹注入低性能rollout组，将盲目探索转化为引导式策略改进。

Result: 在ChartMimic、UniSVG、Design2Code和ScreenBench四个基准上，8B参数模型不仅超越所有开源基线，还达到了与闭源专有模型相当的最优性能。

Conclusion: UniCoder建立了通用视觉到代码合成的新范式，证明了通过精心设计的RL机制可以有效弥合视觉理解与代码生成之间的像素级对齐鸿沟。

Abstract: Visual-to-Code generation, which transforms scientific plots, vector graphics, and webpages into executable scripts, demands a level of pixel-precise alignment that standard Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) fail to achieve through Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) alone. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a theoretical pathway to bridge this gap, its application is hindered by two fundamental obstacles: (1) \textit{Reward Coarseness}, where semantic metrics like CLIP scores fail to penalize fine-grained element deviations, and (2) \textit{Exploration Stagnation}, where the sparse, heterogeneous code search space prevents the policy from bootstrapping valid trajectories. To overcome these limitations, we introduce UniCoder, a unified RL framework that integrates two novel mechanisms. First, we propose \textbf{Symbolic Attribute Alignment}, which employs a lightweight auxiliary LLM to parse generated code into discrete visual attributes (e.g., hex colors, coordinate limits), enabling dense, element-wise reward computation. Second, to escape local optima, we devise \textbf{Reference-Guided Code Optimization}, a strategy that dynamically injects ground-truth trajectories into low-performing rollout groups, transforming blind exploration into guided policy improvement. Extensive experiments on ChartMimic, UniSVG, Design2Code and ScreenBench benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter model not only surpasses all open-source baselines but also achieves state-of-the-art performance comparable to proprietary models, establishing a new paradigm for generalized visual-to-code synthesis.

</details>


### [114] [MemLearner: Learning to Query Context memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31734)
*Jiwen Yu,Jianxiong Gao,Jianhong Bai,Yiran Qin,Kaiyi Huang,Quande Liu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemLearner提出了一种基于学习的自适应上下文查询方法，利用查询令牌连接上下文和预测令牌，通过视频生成模型自身进行上下文查询，提升了视频世界模型在长序列生成中的场景一致性和记忆能力，尤其在遮挡和动态场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型缺乏记忆导致长时生成场景不一致，现有基于规则的上下文帧检索方法在场景遮挡和动态物体情况下泛化能力差。

Method: 提出MemLearner，一种基于学习的自适应上下文查询方法，使用查询令牌桥接上下文令牌和预测令牌；利用预训练视频生成模型自身进行上下文查询（无需额外模块训练）；设计高效训练和推理策略；构建带有场景遮挡和动态物体的长视频数据集（含相机位姿标注）；提出多数据集训练策略，同时利用带标注的渲染视频和无标注的真实视频。

Result: 在场景一致性和记忆能力上显著优于以往的视频世界模型，尤其在遮挡和动态场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: MemLearner通过自适应学习上下文查询有效解决了视频世界模型的长时记忆问题，为未来交互式视频生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Video World Models are interactive video generation models that predict future world states based on user actions and history video frames. A critical challenge in video world models is the lack of memory, causing inconsistent generated scenes over extended durations. Previous methods explored rule-based context frame retrieval as memory, but they fail to generalize in scenarios with scene occlusions and dynamic objects. We propose MemLearner, a learning-based adaptive context query method using query tokens to bridge context and predicted tokens. By leveraging the video generation model itself for context querying, MemLearner exploits pre-trained visual priors without training additional modules from scratch, and incorporates efficient strategies for training and inference. We collect a dataset of long videos with scene occlusions and dynamic objects, paired with camera pose annotations, and propose a multi-dataset training strategy leveraging both annotated rendered and unannotated real-world videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MemLearner significantly outperforms prior video world models in terms of scene consistency and memory, particularly under challenging occlusion and dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [115] [Rhythm-Structured Predictive Learning for Remote Photoplethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31736)
*Ba-Thinh Nguyen,Huu-Dung Nguyen,Thi-Duyen Ngo,Thanh-Ha Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RhythmJEPA提出了一种基于联合嵌入预测学习的rPPG框架，通过预测潜在教师表示、循环节律状态规划器和双顺序Mamba编码器，有效建模脉搏信号的循环时序结构，在多个数据集上取得了竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督rPPG方法倾向于重建掩码像素或低层视觉表征，导致模型偏向面部外观而非潜在生理动态；同时，基于Mamba的方法仅按时间顺序扫描视频token，未能充分利用脉搏信号的循环结构特性。

Method: 1) 联合嵌入预测学习（JEPA）：从掩码面部视频预测潜在教师表征，在嵌入空间促进生理感知表示学习；2) 循环节律状态规划器（CRSP）：估计帧级潜在生理状态，通过带约束转移语法的动态规划解码最可能的循环状态路径；3) 双顺序Mamba编码器（DOM）：结合常规时间顺序扫描和状态顺序扫描；4) 轻量级空间脉冲混合器（SPM）：提取紧凑的脉冲敏感面部token。

Result: 在PURE、UBFC-rPPG和MMPD数据集上，该方法在性能上优于代表性的rPPG方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合嵌入预测学习和显式建模脉搏循环结构，RhythmJEPA能够学习生理感知的表示，为远程生理信号估计提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) estimates physiological signals from facial videos by analyzing subtle pulse induced skin color variations. Despite recent progress, existing self-supervised rPPG methods mainly reconstruct masked pixels or low-level visual representations, which can bias the model toward facial appearance rather than latent physiological dy namics. Moreover, most recent Mamba-based approaches scan facial video tokens only in chronological order, limiting their ability to exploit the cyclic structure of pulse signals. To ad dress these limitations, we propose RhythmJEPA, a rhythm structured joint-embedding predictive learning framework for rPPG. Instead of reconstructing RGB frames, RhythmJEPA predicts latent teacher representations from masked facial videos, thereby encouraging physiology-aware representation learning in the embedding space. To explicitly model pulse-related tem poral structure, we introduce a Cyclic Rhythm-State Plan ner (CRSP), which estimates frame-wise latent physiological states and decodes the most plausible cyclic state path via dynamic programming with a constrained transition grammar. Guided by the decoded states, we further design a Dual Order Mamba Encoder (DOM), which combines conventional chronological scanning with state-ordered scanning to capture both local temporal continuity and long-range rhythm-consistent dependencies. Finally, a lightweight Spatial Pulse Mixer (SPM) extracts compact pulse-sensitive facial tokens with a favorable balance between complexity and performance. Experiments on PURE, UBFC-rPPG, and MMPD show competitive performance over representative rPPG methods. The codes are available at https://github.com/deconasser/RhythmJEPA.

</details>


### [116] [JL1-CC&QA: Extending the JL1-CD Benchmark with Change Captioning and Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31745)
*Ziyuan Liu,Ruifei Zhu,Ouqiao Ma,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了JL1-CC&QA基准数据集，在JL1-CD基础上扩展了变化字幕（CC）和变化问答（QA）两种注释，包含5,000对吉林一号卫星0.5-0.75m分辨率图像，共计17,021条质量验证的字幕和20,060个问答对，通过多模态大模型生成、视觉大模型评判和专家验证的三阶段流程构建，旨在推动遥感变化理解从像素级分割向语义级理解发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥感变化检测仅关注像素级二值分割，能识别变化发生的位置，但无法回答变化是什么以及为什么发生，存在语义鸿沟。

Method: 基于JL1-CD数据集扩展，构建JL1-CC（变化字幕）和JL1-QA（变化问答）两个任务层；采用多模态大模型生成、视觉大模型评判、人类专家验证的三阶段标注流程；包含八种问答类型以支持细粒度交互式地表变化查询。

Result: 生成17,021条质量验证的变化字幕和20,060个问答对，涵盖8种问题类型。

Conclusion: JL1-CC&QA作为统一变化掩码、变化字幕和变化问答的基准数据集，有望成为推动遥感多任务变化理解的重要资源。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection (CD) traditionally focuses on pixel-level binary segmentation, which identifies where changes occur but neither what nor why. To bridge this semantic gap, we introduce JL1-CC&QA, a multi-task benchmark that extends the JL1-CD dataset with two complementary annotation layers: change captioning (CC) and change question answering (QA). Built upon 5,000 bi-temporal image pairs acquired by the Jilin-1 satellite at 0.5-0.75m ground sample distance, the benchmark comprises: (i) JL1-CC, providing 17,021 quality-verified captions that describe diverse land-cover transformations; and (ii) JL1-QA, offering 20,060 question-answer pairs across eight question types, enabling fine-grained, interactive interrogation of surface changes. All annotations are produced via a three-stage pipeline consisting of multi-modal large language model (LLM) generation, vision-grounded LLM judging, and human expert verification. We hope that JL1-CC&QA, as a benchmark unifying binary change masks, change captions, and change-oriented QA over the same image set, will serve as a valuable resource for the community to advance multi-task change understanding in remote sensing. The dataset is available at https://github.com/circleLZY/JL1-CD.

</details>


### [117] [Estimating Velocity of Spheres from Rolling-Shutter Image(s)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31760)
*Wenjie Xue,Jun Yang,Jingmin Wang,Limin Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用卷帘快门畸变作为时间信息源,从单帧图像中估计高速运动球状物体的三维平移和角速度,提出无需特征对应的公式和两阶段优化方法,在合成和真实数据集上实现了准确鲁棒的运动参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将卷帘快门畸变视为需要校正的伪影,而本文反其道而行之,将其作为宝贵的时间信息源,用于从单帧图像中估计高速运动物体的三维运动参数。

Method: 设计可检测的球形图案;提出免对应点的公式,通过反向投影框架中的几何一致性约束恢复运动;利用球体几何特性将平移和旋转运动解耦,通过两阶段优化分别估计线速度和角速度。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明,该方法在挑战性的高速条件下能够准确鲁棒地估计运动参数。

Conclusion: 卷帘快门畸变可以作为有效的时间信息源,用于从单帧图像中恢复高速运动物体的三维运动参数,该方法对无纹理物体同样有效。

Abstract: Rolling-shutter cameras introduce characteristic distortions when imaging fast moving objects, and these effects are typically treated as artifacts to be corrected. In this work, we instead leverage rolling-shutter distortions as a valuable source of temporal information to estimate the 3D translational and angular velocities of rapidly moving spherical objects from a single rolling-shutter frame. We design a robust and easily detectable spherical pattern and propose a correspondence-free formulation that recovers motion by enforcing geometric consistency in a back-projection framework. By exploiting the geometry of the sphere, translational and rotational motions are decoupled and estimated through a two-stage optimization process, enabling reliable velocity recovery even for textureless objects. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate accurate and robust estimation of motion parameters under challenging high-speed conditions.

</details>


### [118] [Mesh BDF: Barycentric Dominance Field for 3D Native Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31777)
*Gaochao Song,Haohan Weng,Luo Zhang,Zibo Zhao,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Baycentric Dominance Field (BDF)，一种定义在三角网格表面的连续表示方法，将顶点拓扑连接转换为连续表面信号，弥合了离散网格拓扑与连续扩散生成模型之间的鸿沟，使得扩散模型能够生成更高质量、更大规模和更强鲁棒性的原生3D网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在原生3D网格生成中虽取得进展，但其固有约束严重限制了生成网格的面数、顶点分辨率以及纹理支持能力，亟需新的方法突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 提出Baycentric Dominance Field (BDF)，一种定义在三角网格表面的连续表示，将离散的顶点拓扑连接编码为连续表面信号，利用其与纹理图的相似性，无缝集成到现有3D扩散管道中，无需修改模型架构。

Result: 实验表明，BDF赋能的扩散模型在生成网格质量、可扩展性和鲁棒性上均显著优于当前最先进的自回归方法。

Conclusion: BDF通过连接离散拓扑与连续生成建模，有效克服了自回归范式在网格生成中的局限性，为高质量、大规模原生3D网格生成提供了新路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) modeling has recently achieved remarkable progress in native 3D mesh generation, largely due to its natural ability to handle variable-length, discrete data structures. However, the inherent constraints of the AR paradigm severely restrict the generated meshes, leading to limited face counts, bounded vertex resolutions, and difficulties in supporting textures. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose the Barycentric Dominance Field (BDF), a continuous representation defined on triangular mesh surfaces that elegantly encodes vertex topological connectivity. BDF bridges the fundamental gap between discrete mesh topology and continuous diffusion-based generative modeling by transforming connectivity into a continuous surface signal. As an intrinsic mesh property, BDF shares strong similarities with texture maps, enabling its seamless integration into existing 3D diffusion pipelines without requiring architectural modifications. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDF empowers diffusion models to generate native meshes with significantly higher quality, greater scalability, and stronger robustness compared to state-of-the-art autoregressive methods.

</details>


### [119] [Self-Supervised Temporal Regularization for Landmark-Based Cardiac Segmentation with Automatic AHA Regional Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31785)
*David Montalvo-García,Nicolás Gaggion,María J. Ledesma-Carbayo,Enzo Ferrante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自监督时间正则化作为后训练精调阶段，利用图像序列的时间连贯性实现心脏分割和运动估计的时间一致性，并自动将地标映射到AHA 17节段临床标准，在CAMUS数据集上验证了临床效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的心脏分割模型在独立帧上训练会产生时间不连续性，影响临床测量的可靠性，尤其是在心脏超声图像序列中。

Method: 引入自监督时间正则化作为后训练精调阶段，通过惩罚连续帧间的速度和加速度不连续性来强制时间一致的分割和运动估计，并利用学习到的解剖对应关系自动将地标映射到AHA 17节段标准。

Result: 在CAMUS数据集上验证了结合时间一致性和自动区域映射的临床效用。

Conclusion: 自监督时间正则化能有效提升心脏超声分割的时间一致性，结合自动区域映射有助于标准化临床评估和病理运动模式检测。

Abstract: Graph-based cardiac segmentation with implicit anatomical correspondences provides topological guarantees and population-level analysis capabilities, but models trained on independent frames of image sequences exhibit temporal discontinuities that affect reliable clinical measurements, particularly in cardiac ultrasound. In this work, we introduce self-supervised temporal regularization as a post-training refinement stage that exploits the temporal coherence in image sequences to enforce consistent cardiac segmentation and motion estimation over time, without requiring per-frame annotations. By penalizing velocity and acceleration discontinuities across consecutive frames, our method achieves temporally consistent segmentations while maintaining the learned anatomical correspondences. We further leverage these correspondences to automatically map landmarks to the AHA 17-segment clinical standard, enabling standardized regional assessment and detection of pathological myocardial motion patterns. Validation on CAMUS dataset demonstrates the clinical utility of combining temporal consistency with automatic regional mapping. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/david-montalvoo/MaskHybridGNet-TempReg

</details>


### [120] [Breaking Failure Cascades: Step-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Medical Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31825)
*Junha Jung,Minbyul Jeong,Suhyeon Lim,Sungwook Jung,Jaehoon Yun,Taeyun Roh,Mujeen Sung,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MRPO（医学推理感知策略优化），一种引入步骤级过程奖励的强化学习算法，通过给早期错误推理步骤分配指数级更大的惩罚来打破错误级联，显著提升医学多模态大模型的推理质量与最终答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大模型后训练流程主要依赖最终答案正确性或序列级偏好，导致稀疏的信用分配问题，难以优化临床应用中至关重要的推理过程。分析发现，早期推理阶段的级联错误是医学VQA中错误预测的主要原因。

Method: 提出MRPO算法，在RL框架中引入步骤级过程奖励：当最终答案错误时，对早期无效推理步骤的token分配指数级更大的惩罚，从而打破失败级联而不影响成功的推理路径。

Result: 在三个多模态LLM骨干上，MRPO一致优于标准GRPO和近期RL基线；在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上甚至超越更大的医学MLLM（HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B）2.79个点；早期推理失败率从64.0%降至13.0%。

Conclusion: 针对性缓解级联失败既能提升推理质量，也能提高最终答案准确性，验证了步骤级过程奖励在医学多模态推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models have shown great promise in clinical image reasoning, but existing post-training pipelines remain predominantly outcome-centric, relying on final answer correctness or sequence-level preferences. This suffers from sparse credit assignment, making it difficult to optimize the reasoning process essential for clinical applications. Our analysis reveals that cascading errors from early-stage reasoning failures are a leading cause of incorrect predictions in medical visual question answering (VQA) benchmarks. Motivated by this, we propose Medical Reasoning-aware Policy Optimization (MRPO), an RL algorithm that incorporates step-wise process rewards. When the final answer is incorrect, MRPO assigns exponentially larger penalties to tokens in earlier invalid reasoning steps, breaking failure cascades without compromising successful paths. Across three multimodal LLM backbones, MRPO consistently outperforms standard GRPO and a recent RL baseline, and on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct even surpasses substantially larger medical MLLMs such as HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B by 2.79 points. Moreover, MRPO reduces early-stage reasoning failures from 64.0% to 13.0%, showing that targeted mitigation of cascading failures improves both reasoning quality and final answer accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/dmis-lab/MRPO

</details>


### [121] [MuSViT: A Foundation Vision Model for Sheet Music Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31811)
*Carlos Penarrubia,Antonio Rios-Vila,Eliseo Fuentes-Martinez,Juan C. Martinez-Sevilla,Francisco J. Castellanos,María Alfaro-Contreras,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MuSViT，首个用于乐谱表示的基础视觉模型——通过在IMSLP的970万页数据上进行掩码自编码器预训练的ViT编码器。采用两阶段课程训练（合成乐谱预热+大规模真实语料），在四项下游任务中，线性探测下显著优于通用视觉编码器，微调下超越任务特定SOTA方法，且嵌入空间直接编码了符号音乐结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在视觉和语言处理中取得了巨大成功，但乐谱作为一种视觉化的音乐语言编码，缺乏类似的领域特定骨干网络。现有通用视觉编码器无法充分捕捉乐谱的符号化结构化属性，亟需一个专门针对乐谱理解的基础模型。

Method: 采用ViT编码器，通过掩码自编码器（MAE）进行预训练。训练采用两阶段课程策略：第一阶段在合成排版乐谱上预热，第二阶段在IMSLP的970万页真实乐谱语料上进行大规模训练。评估采用线性探测（冻结编码器）和微调两种范式，涵盖全页/谱行乐谱识别、音乐符号检测和难度分类四项任务。

Result: 线性探测下，MuSViT持续优于通用视觉编码器（包括大规模模型），表明通用表示在乐谱的结构化符号属性上存在系统性不足。微调下，MuSViT普遍超越任务特定SOTA方法。嵌入-转录一致性分析显示，MuSViT的嵌入空间直接编码了音乐符号结构，而其他编码器的嵌入与乐谱内容无关。

Conclusion: MuSViT可作为乐谱理解的基础骨干网络，填补了该领域缺乏专用基础视觉模型的空白，其嵌入空间对音乐符号结构的编码能力使其区别于通用视觉模型。

Abstract: Foundation models have transformed vision and language processing by providing rich, reusable representations that transfer across diverse tasks. Sheet music, as a visual encoding of musical language, lacks such a strong domain-specific backbone. We introduce MuSViT (Music Score Vision Transformer): the first foundation vision model for sheet music representation -- a ViT encoder pre-trained via Masked Autoencoders on 9.7 million pages from the IMSLP. To handle the complexity of real-world scores, we adopt a two-stage curriculum: a synthetic warm-up on typeset scores followed by large-scale training on the full IMSLP corpus. We evaluate MuSViT on four downstream tasks -- full-page and staff-level music score recognition, music symbol detection, and score difficulty classification -- under two scenarios: linear probing (frozen encoder) and fine-tuning. Under linear probing, MuSViT consistently outperforms modern vision encoders, revealing that general-purpose representations, regardless of scale, fall systematically short on the structured symbolic properties of musical notation. Under fine-tuning, MuSViT generally improves upon task-specific state-of-the-art methods. An additional embedding-transcription consistency analysis reveals that MuSViT encodes symbolic musical structure directly in its representation space -- unlike other encoders, whose embeddings do not correlate with music notation content. These results establish MuSViT as a foundation backbone for sheet music understanding.

</details>


### [122] [Real-Time Source-Free Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31834)
*Sairam VCR,Varun Gopal,Poornima Jain,Vineeth N Balasubramanian,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RT-SFOD方法，基于YOLOv10双头检测器，通过双头伪标签融合(DHF)和多尺度自适应表示多样性损失(MARD)实现高效源域自由目标检测，在速度、精度和模型大小上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源域自由目标检测(SFOD)方法依赖重量级架构，仅追求精度而忽视了延迟和内存约束，无法满足自动驾驶、监控和机器人等实际场景在域迁移下的实时需求。

Method: 提出RT-SFOD方法，包含两个核心模块：(1) 双头伪标签融合(DHF)，选择性融合O2O和O2M头的预测结果，保留精度并恢复遗漏目标；(2) 多尺度自适应表示多样性损失(MARD)，通过对多尺度特征图施加检测感知的方差和协方差约束，缓解域迁移导致的多尺度特征判别性退化。

Result: 在域迁移基准上，相比现有最优SFOD方法，mAP提升1.4-3.5%，吞吐量提高1.3倍，参数量减少约2倍。

Conclusion: 速度-精度-模型大小三者之间的权衡并非必要，通过轻量级双头检测器结合针对性设计，可以在保持高效推理的同时达到最优的域适应性能。

Abstract: Real-world detectors for autonomous driving, surveillance, and robotics must handle domain-shifts under strict latency and memory constraints, yet existing source-free object detection (SFOD) methods rely on heavyweight architectures that prioritize accuracy alone. We show this trade-off is unnecessary: building on YOLOv10, an NMS-free dual-head detector, we achieve state-of-the-art adaptation accuracy while being faster and more compact. We observe that directly applying vanilla mean-teacher self-training to dual-head detectors leads to suboptimal adaptation performance due to two key factors. First, simple pseudo-label generation strategies, such as using a single head or directly combining high-confidence predictions from both heads, yield suboptimal supervision under domain-shift. We propose DHF (Dual-Head Pseudo-Label Fusion) which selectively admits one-to-one (O2O) and one-to-many (O2M) head predictions, preserving precision and recovering missed objects. Second, we observe domain-shift collapses multi-scale feature discriminability. We propose the use of our MARD (Multi-scale Adaptive Representation Diversification) loss which mitigates this by enforcing detection-aware variance and covariance constraints on multi-scale feature maps. Both modules are training-time only, leaving inference unchanged. Across domain-shift benchmarks, our method, RT-SFOD yields 1.4 to 3.5\% mAP gains, 1.3$\times$ higher throughput, with $\sim$2$\times$ fewer parameters than prior state-of-the-art SFOD methods, thus advancing the Pareto frontier of the speed-accuracy-model size trade-off. We report main results with YOLOv10, and demonstrate generalizability with additional YOLO- and DETR-based dual-head detectors. Code is available here: https://github.com/Sairam13001/RT-SFOD/

</details>


### [123] [Generative Lane Topology Reasoning via Autoregressive Model with Geometry Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31814)
*Jiahui Fu,Zehao Huang,Han Li,Naiyan Wang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoGPT提出一种生成式框架，通过自回归序列建模学习车道图结构的几何先验，将车道图序列化为离散token并预训练车道序列Transformer，再通过感知适配器将BEV特征对齐到预训练场景条件，在OpenLane-V2上显著提升车道级和点级指标，生成几何一致且结构完整的车道图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法采用检测+关联范式独立处理每个车道实例，导致连接端点几何不一致，且因视觉遮挡产生不完整图结构。

Method: 1) 构建330万场景的大规模地图数据集；2) 车道分词器将车道图序列化为离散token，场景上下文编码器提取全局场景token；3) 预训练自回归车道序列Transformer，通过场景条件下一token预测学习几何先验；4) 感知适配器将多视图BEV特征与预训练场景条件对齐，迁移几何先验到基于传感器的车道图预测。

Result: 在OpenLane-V2上，车道级指标平均提升+6.4，点级指标平均提升+11.6，生成几何一致且结构完整的车道图。

Conclusion: TopoGPT通过生成式建模和学习几何先验，有效克服了检测+关联范式的几何不一致和结构不完整问题。

Abstract: Lane topology reasoning aims to construct a lane graph from onboard sensor observations. Existing methods follow a detection and association paradigm that treats each lane instance independently, leading to geometric inconsistency at connected endpoints and incomplete graphs due to visual occlusions. To address these issues, we propose TopoGPT, a generative framework that learns the geometry prior from typical lane graph structures through autoregressive sequence modeling. Specifically, we construct a large-scale map dataset comprising 3.3M scenes. For each lane graph, a lane tokenizer serializes it into discrete tokens, while a scene context encoder converts it into a rasterized image and extracts global features as scene tokens. We pre-train an autoregressive lane sequence transformer via scene-conditioned next-token prediction, endowing the model with the geometry prior over lane graph structures. Building upon this prior, a perception adapter aligns BEV features from multi-view images with the pre-trained scene condition, transferring the learned geometry prior to sensor-based lane graph prediction. On the OpenLane-V2 benchmark, TopoGPT outperforms existing methods by an average of +6.4 on lane-level and +11.6 on point-level metrics, and produces geometrically consistent and structurally complete lane graphs.

</details>


### [124] [Attend, Transform, or Silence: Operator-Level Visual Skipping for Efficient Multimodal LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31903)
*Zhaoyang Luo,Runmin Dong,Miao Yang,Fan Wei,Yushan Lai,Bin Luo,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种运算符级视觉令牌跳过框架，在保留完整视觉令牌序列的情况下，选择性跳过冗余的注意力或FFN运算，在Qwen3-VL上减少33.7% TFLOPs并保留99.5%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM加速方法通常粗粒度地移除视觉令牌或跳过整层更新，可能丢弃细粒度证据或抑制有用运算。本文从答案可观测视角发现视觉令牌更新存在"答案静默冗余"。

Method: 将每个Transformer层分解为注意力与FFN算子，提出运算符级视觉令牌跳过框架，根据冗余程度选择性跳过注意力、FFN或两者同时跳过，保留完整视觉令牌序列。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL上减少33.7% TFLOPs，保留99.5%的原始模型性能；在三种MLLM架构和10个VQA基准上取得强效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 细粒度的运算符级视觉令牌跳过比粗粒度的整层跳过或令牌移除更有效，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly process long visual-token sequences, increasing the overall inference computation. Existing acceleration methods usually remove visual tokens or skip visual-token updates in entire layers, but these coarse strategies may discard fine-grained evidence or suppress useful operators together with redundant ones. In this paper, we study visual-token computation from an answer-observable perspective and find that late visual-token updates can remain large while having little effect on answer-token representations. Motivated by this answer-silent redundancy, we decompose each Transformer layer into attention and FFN operators and show that useful visual computation is often operator-dominant and layer-dependent. We propose an operator-level visual-token skipping framework that preserves the full visual-token sequence while selectively bypassing redundant attention, FFN, or both. Experiments across three MLLM architectures and 10 VQA benchmarks show that our method achieves strong efficiency-accuracy trade-offs, reducing \textbf{33.7\%} TFLOPs on Qwen3-VL while retaining \textbf{99.5\%} of the vanilla model performance.

</details>


### [125] [Absorption-Feature-Guided Distance-Decoupled Estimation and Band Selection for LWIR Hyperspectral Passive Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31824)
*Shuo Liu,Chen Fan,Zhihe Chen,Xiaolin Huang,Lilian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ADER框架，利用长波红外高光谱数据中的大气吸收特征进行被动测距，通过B样条发射率解耦、臭氧吸收分类、贪婪波段选择等策略，在保持高精度的同时实现约两个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长波红外高光谱观测中包含与距离相关的大气吸收特征，为被动测距提供了物理基础。然而，由于距离信息与温度、发射率、路径辐射等非线性耦合，距离反演是病态问题。现有全波段逐像素联合优化方法计算量巨大，且未充分利用大气吸收的尖锐结构。

Method: 1) 用B样条控制点表示发射率并施加平滑先验，抑制对大气吸收结构的过拟合，实现距离解耦估计；2) 利用臭氧吸收线索将像素分为发射主导和反射主导两类；3) 发射主导像素通过一维吸收残差最小化估计距离；4) 反射主导像素通过完整辐射模型的下行辐射补偿来优化初始估计；5) 基于多场景有效Fisher信息设计贪婪波段选择策略，减少光谱冗余。

Result: 在真实场景实验中，ADER在全集和20波段设置下均能恢复与LiDAR一致的空间距离结构，提升了测距精度，且相较于公开的全波段高光谱测距方法实现了约两个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: ADER通过解耦策略和波段选择，有效解决了长波红外高光谱被动测距的病态问题，实现了高效、高精度的距离估计。

Abstract: Long-wave infrared (LWIR) hyperspectral observations contain distance-dependent atmospheric absorption signatures, providing a physical basis for long-range passive ranging. However, in natural scenes, these signatures are nonlinearly coupled with target temperature, material emissivity, and path radiance, making distance inversion from observed radiance ill posed. Existing methods typically rely on full-band measurements and pixel-wise joint optimization, which is computationally expensive and does not explicitly exploit sharp atmospheric absorption structures. This paper proposes an Absorption-Guided Distance-Decoupled Estimation and Refinement (ADER) framework for LWIR hyperspectral passive ranging. ADER represents emissivity with B-spline control points under a smoothness prior, suppressing overfitting to atmospheric absorption structures and enabling distance-decoupled estimation. It further uses ozone-absorption cues to classify pixels into emission-dominant and reflection-dominant groups. For emission-dominant pixels, ADER compensates path radiance and transmittance and estimates distance by one-dimensional absorption-residual minimization. For reflection-dominant pixels, ADER refines the initial estimate using downwelling-radiance compensation based on the complete radiative model. To reduce spectral redundancy, ADER also introduces a greedy band selection strategy based on multi-scene effective Fisher information for the distance parameter. Experiments on real scenes show that ADER recovers LiDAR-consistent spatial distance structures under both full-band and 20-band settings, improves ranging accuracy in the evaluated regions, and achieves approximately two orders of magnitude speedup over a public full-band hyperspectral ranging method.

</details>


### [126] [LUNA: Learning Universal 3D Human Animation Beyond Skinning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31981)
*Peng Li,Rawal Khirodkar,Junxuan Li,Yuan Dong,Chen Cao,Yuan Liu,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo,Shunsuke Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUNA是一个无需线性混合蒙皮(LBS)的通用神经动画模型，通过transformer运动回归器将多种2D控制(图像、关键点、草图等)直接映射为3D高斯变形，结合混合监督策略(来自LBS教师模型的软先验+未标注视频损失)，实现逼真的3D人体动画和零样本跨身份泛化，是首个支持隐式2D驱动的端到端3D可动画化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LBS和参数化人体模型的3D化身方法受限于表达力不足和拟合伪影，且依赖显式身体拟合，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出无需LBS的通用神经动画模型LUNA：核心是transformer运动回归器，解耦全局刚体运动与局部非刚性细节；采用混合监督，从LBS教师模型蒸馏软结构先验，并结合有标签数据和无标签视频损失进行训练。

Result: LUNA在视觉保真度上媲美LBS方法，同时实现逼真人体运动和零样本跨身份泛化，支持多种驱动模态。

Conclusion: LUNA是首个无需LBS、支持隐式2D驱动的端到端3D可动画化模型，突破了传统方法的表达限制和泛化瓶颈。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic, animatable 3D human avatars from monocular images still largely depends on Linear Blend Skinning (LBS) and parametric body models, which constrain expressivity and often introduce artifacts due to imperfect fitting. We propose LUNA, an LBS-free universal neural animation model that directly maps multiple 2D controls like images, keypoints, sketches, and unseen characters into 3D Gaussian deformations, bypassing explicit body fitting. At its core, a transformer-based motion regressor disentangles global rigid motion from fine-grained local dynamics to capture both coherent movement and subtle non-rigid effects. To resolve the inherent ambiguity of 2D-to-3D lifting while scaling beyond fitted datasets, we introduce hybrid supervision that distills soft structural priors from an LBS teacher and a loss that supports training on both limited fitted data and large in-the-wild unlabeled videos. Extensive experiments show LUNA achieves competitive visual fidelity compared to LBS-based approaches, while delivering realistic human motion and zero-shot cross-identity generalization across diverse driving modalities. To the best of our knowledge, LUNA is the first end-to-end 3D animatable model that supports implicit 2D driving.

</details>


### [127] [FLORA: A deep learning approach to predict forest attributes from heterogeneous LiDAR data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32023)
*Emilie Vautier,Clément Mallet,Cédric Vega*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FLORA深度学习框架，利用异构LiDAR点云和辅助数据预测六种森林属性（优势木高、总蓄积量、落叶林蓄积量、针叶林蓄积量、胸高断面积、立木密度）。基于法国32,052个NFI样地训练，单一模型在叶盛/落叶期数据上表现优于分季节模型，优势木高rRMSE≈12.3%（R²=0.88），总蓄积量rRMSE≈39%（R²=0.74）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家LiDAR项目在采集条件（传感器、飞行参数、季节、扫描角）上存在异质性，导致现有局部校准模型在泛化至全国尺度时鲁棒性不足，亟需能够处理异构LiDAR数据的大规模森林属性估计方法。

Method: 提出FLORA框架：1）八叉树（octree）骨干网络处理异构LiDAR点云；2）通过晚期融合门控机制（late-fusion gating mechanism）整合生态和时空辅助变量；3）在法国LiDAR HD项目的32,052个NFI样地上训练单一模型，同时覆盖叶盛和落叶期数据。

Result: 单一跨季节模型优于分季节专用模型，提升跨季节鲁棒性；辅助变量对总体预测增益适中，但对树种特异性蓄积量贡献显著；优势木高rRMSE≈12.3%（R²=0.88），总蓄积量rRMSE≈39%（R²=0.74）。

Conclusion: FLORA为异构国家LiDAR项目的大尺度森林属性估计提供了鲁棒基线，证明了单一跨季节模型的可行性和辅助变量在树种区分中的价值。

Abstract: Forest attributes are essential for national-scale resource monitoring. Airborne LiDAR metrics are among the auxiliary variables most strongly correlated with forest attributes used in National Forest Inventory (NFI) estimates. However, producing wall-to-wall predictions remains challenging when LiDAR data are acquired under heterogeneous conditions. As national LiDAR programs expand across Europe, variability in sensors, flight parameters, seasons, and scan angles limits the robustness of existing models, which are often calibrated for local conditions. We present FLORA (Forest LiDAR Octree Regression with Auxiliary Data), a deep learning framework that predicts six forest attributes: dominant height, total volume, deciduous volume, coniferous volume, basal area, and stem density from heterogeneous LiDAR point clouds. FLORA combines an octree-based backbone with ecological and spatiotemporal auxiliary variables through a late-fusion gating mechanism. Models are trained and evaluated on 32,052 National Forest Inventory plots across mainland France using data from the French LiDAR HD program. A single model trained on both leaf-on and leaf-off acquisitions outperforms season-specific models and improves cross-season robustness. Auxiliary variables provide modest overall gains but contribute more strongly to species-specific volume prediction. FLORA achieves an rRMSE of about 12.3% (R2 = 0.88) for dominant height and 39% (R2 = 0.74) for total volume, providing a robust baseline for large-scale forest attribute estimation from heterogeneous national LiDAR programs.

</details>


### [128] [PriorEye: Geospatial Visual Priors for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31830)
*Kyuhwan Yeon,Benjamin Ramtoula,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于地理空间视觉先验的端到端自动驾驶记忆增强模块，通过双记忆架构和自适应门控机制，在NAVSIM-v2基准测试上持续提升多种基线模型的性能，并增强了对传感器损坏的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法仅依赖实时传感器观测，缺乏人类驾驶员通过先验经验获得的预期性前馈能力，导致行为反应式而非预见式。

Method: 提出地理空间视觉先验（geospatial visual priors），将其锚定到预定行驶路线上；设计记忆增强模块，包含双记忆架构（上下文记忆+持久回退记忆）和自适应记忆门控，可灵活集成到现有端到端方法中。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v2基准测试上，该方法持续提升了多种端到端基线模型的性能；同时增强了传感器损坏情况下的鲁棒性，并在检索先验不可靠时通过双记忆设计提供安全回退。

Conclusion: 地理空间视觉先验能有效为自动驾驶提供独立于实时传感器的视觉空间预判能力，双记忆架构与自适应门控是实现这一能力的有效技术方案。

Abstract: Most end-to-end autonomous driving methods rely solely on instantaneous sensor observations, limiting them to reactive behavior without the anticipatory foresight human drivers employ through prior experience. We introduce geospatial visual priors, street-level visual context anchored to the intended driving route, providing visual-spatial foresight independent of real-time sensors. We propose a memory augmentation module featuring a dual-memory architecture and an adaptive memory gate, which can be easily integrated into existing end-to-end approaches. This design pairs a contextual memory for retrieved priors with a persistent fallback memory, and dynamically regulates the influence of memories based on current state compatibility. Evaluated on the NAVSIM-v2 benchmark, our approach consistently improves performance across diverse end-to-end baselines. Furthermore, because these priors are independent of onboard sensors, our method inherently improves robustness against sensor corruption, while the dual-memory design ensures safe fallback when the retrieved priors themselves become unreliable. Our project page is available at https://ori-mrg.github.io/PriorEye.

</details>


### [129] [Towards Voxel Spacing Consistency for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31839)
*Xin You,Runze Yang,Minghui Zhang,Hanxiao Zhang,Han Li,Yi Yu,Jie Yang,Nassir Navab,Yun Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Consispace，一种语义感知的重采样框架，通过ODE约束保持层间解剖连续性和预训练模型特征重加权保持层内语义一致性，作为预处理步骤可提升医学体分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体医学图像分割方法过度关注分割架构设计，忽视了体素间距这一物理属性。图像重采样作为分割前的普遍预处理步骤，其与分割任务之间的交互关系未被系统性研究。

Method: 提出Consispace框架：(1) 基于ODE的解剖约束，通过连续插值器建模层间动力学，实现复杂解剖过渡下的忠实重建；(2) 利用预训练视觉模型提取稠密特征构建层内语义相关图，通过特征重加权注入类别级语义一致性；(3) 将层间和层内约束集成到隐式神经网络中，支持任意尺度重采样。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，Consispace在重建质量和感知保真度上表现优越，生成更平滑的层间解剖结构，作为预处理步骤能有效提升下游分割性能。

Conclusion: Consispace通过语义感知的重采样框架系统性地利用了重采样与分割之间的交互关系，证明了在预处理阶段引入解剖和语义约束对提升分割效果的重要性。

Abstract: Volumetric medical image segmentation is essential for both preoperative diagnosis and intraoperative guidance. While recent years have witnessed rapid progress in segmentation architectures, comparatively little attention is paid to the physical voxel spacing of anatomical data. Indeed, volumetric image resampling is a ubiquitous preprocessing step before segmentation, yet its interaction with downstream segmentation has not been systematically exploited. In this work, we study the correlation between image resampling and segmentation, and propose Consispace, a semantic-aware resampling framework that achieves consistent voxel spacing in the axial direction while preserving anatomical and semantic consistency. Consispace introduces an ODE-based anatomical constraint to model inter-slice dynamics with a continuous interpolator, enabling faithful reconstruction under complex anatomical transitions beyond discrete interpolation. To further couple resampling with segmentation objectives, we leverage dense features from a pretrained vision model to build intra-slice semantic correlation maps and inject class-wise semantic consistency via feature reweighting during resampling. Both intra-slice and inter-slice constraints are integrated into an implicit neural network, supporting arbitrary-scale resampling. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that Consispace achieves superior reconstruction quality and perceptual fidelity, produces smoother inter-slice anatomy, and improves downstream segmentation performance when used as a preprocessing step.

</details>


### [130] [SENSE-VAD: Sentient and Semantic Video Anomaly Detection for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31875)
*Nghia T. Nguyen,Lokman Bekit,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SENSE-VAD，首个专为自动驾驶设计的社会性复杂异常检测合成视频基准。通过CARLA和Unreal Engine生成包含个体行为、群体行为、人-物交互、骑行者交互、车辆与代理等多类别的异常场景，并严格将社会性异常与基于运动或外观的异常分离。实验表明现有SOTA方法无法有效解决这一独特挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶异常检测方法仅关注运动统计层面的异常，忽视了由代理间关系构成的社会性复杂情境（如儿童逃离监护人、人被抱持、追逐等），这些情境在运动信号上并不突出但在社会语境中明显异常。

Method: 基于CARLA模拟器和Unreal Engine生成合成视频数据集，涵盖个体行为、群体行为、人-物交互、骑行者交互、车辆与代理等多个异常类别，提供逐帧二值标注。核心设计原则是将社会性异常与基于运动或外观的异常分离，并附带真实世界视频作为sim-to-real迁移探针。

Result: 对现有SOTA视频异常检测基线进行评估，证明社会性复杂异常构成了一个当前尚未解决的独特挑战，现有方法无法有效检测此类异常。

Conclusion: 社会性复杂异常是自动驾驶安全中一个被忽视但关键的维度，需要开发专门表征代理间关系异常的检测方法。数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) must navigate not only motion-based hazards but also socially complex situations whose danger is constituted by inter-agent relationships rather than movement statistics alone. A child running away from a guardian, a person being carried by another, or a pursuer chasing a pedestrian across a sidewalk are all anomalous in social context, yet none produces an obvious motion signal that current anomaly detectors are equipped to flag. We introduce SENSE-VAD, the first synthetic video anomaly detection benchmark for autonomous driving explicitly designed around socially complex anomalies. Using the CARLA simulator and Unreal Engine (UE), we generate distinct anomaly scenarios across multiple categories: individual behaviors, group behaviors, person--object interactions, cyclist interactions, vehicle & agent, each annotated with per-frame binary labels. A key design principle is the separation of social anomaly from motion-based or appearance-based anomaly: many scenarios involve motion of objects that appears unremarkable in isolation but is anomalous in relational context. We additionally provide real-world normal and anomalous videos as a sim-to-real transfer probe. We evaluate state-of-the-art video anomaly detection baselines and demonstrate that socially complex anomalies constitute a distinct and currently unsolved challenge. Our dataset, annotations, and generation code are publicly available.

</details>


### [131] [RESOLVE: A Multi-Resolution and Multi-Modal Dataset for Roadside Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31895)
*Shaozu Ding,Linan Song,Marco De Vincenzi,Dajiang Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RESOLVE，一个大规模真实世界路边多分辨率LiDAR与相机融合基准数据集，系统评估了在LiDAR点云稀疏度变化下，单模态与融合架构在3D检测与跟踪任务中的表现。数据集包含10万+图像、2.6万点云帧、22万标注框，覆盖3种LiDAR分辨率、多种光照天气条件及10类交通参与者。实验揭示了多模态融合如何补偿LiDAR点稀疏性，为设计成本高效的路边多模态感知系统提供线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路边协同感知研究中，LiDAR已被广泛集成到交通摄像头中以扩展覆盖并缓解遮挡问题，但单模态与相机-LiDAR融合架构在不同LiDAR点稀疏度（由传感器配置和场景依赖的感知条件引起）下的行为尚未被充分探索。因此，需要一个支持系统评估的基准数据集。

Method: 构建RESOLVE数据集：在真实城市交叉口采集，包含100k+图像、26k点云帧、220k手动标注边界框，覆盖10类交通参与者。数据集支持三种LiDAR分辨率级别（在固定其他感知和环境因素下）的控制变量实验，允许公平地跨架构比较点云分布偏移（分辨率变化、感知距离、训练-推理分辨率不匹配）带来的影响。

Result: 大量基准实验揭示了多模态融合如何补偿LiDAR点稀疏性，为设计成本高效的路边多模态感知系统提供了线索。

Conclusion: RESOLVE数据集及其基准实验为路边3D检测与跟踪中不同LiDAR分辨率条件下的单模态与融合架构提供了系统评估，表明多模态融合可有效缓解点云稀疏问题，对实际部署具有指导意义。

Abstract: LiDAR has increasingly been integrated into traffic cameras to expand coverage and mitigate occlusion in roadside cooperative perception. However, how unimodal and camera-LiDAR fusion architectures behave under variations in LiDAR point sparsity induced by sensor configurations and scene-dependent sensing conditions remains underexplored. We introduce RESOLVE, a large-scale real-world benchmark dataset featuring multi-resolution roadside LiDAR and synchronized camera-LiDAR sensing for systematic evaluation of unimodal and fusion-based architectures in roadside 3D detection and tracking. RESOLVE contains over 100k images and 26k point cloud frames with 220k manually annotated bounding boxes, captured at a real-world urban intersection across diverse lighting and weather conditions and spanning 10 classes of traffic participants. In particular, RESOLVE enables controlled evaluation across three LiDAR resolution levels while keeping all other sensing and environmental factors fixed. This allows fair cross-architecture comparisons under point cloud distribution shifts resulting from resolution variations, sensing distance, and training-inference resolution mismatches. Results from extensive benchmark experiments reveal insights into how multimodal fusion can compensate for LiDAR point sparsity, offering clues for designing cost-efficient roadside multimodal perception. The dataset and benchmark codes are available at https://github.com/ASU-Suo-Lab/RESOLVE.

</details>


### [132] [DriveWeaver: Point-Conditioned Video Inpainting for Controllable Vehicle Insertion in Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31918)
*Junzhe Jiang,Zipei Ma,Zijie Pan,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveWeaver是一种基于视频修复的框架，通过点云条件生成前景车辆并实现与背景的平滑融合，用于自动驾驶仿真中的可控车辆插入，支持长时生成和实时渲染，在视觉真实感和几何一致性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预重建的3D资产，导致插入的前景与背景之间光照不一致，且受限于有限的手工3D资产，难以大规模部署。

Method: 提出DriveWeaver框架，对掩码目标插入区域执行基于车辆点云条件的视频修复，生成高质量时序一致的车辆；采用全局到局部的层级修复策略支持长时生成并保持身份和外观一致性；通过城市重建管线提取显式3D高斯表示实现实时渲染。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在视觉真实感和几何一致性上优于现有基线，为可扩展的自动驾驶场景增强提供了稳健工具。

Conclusion: DriveWeaver通过视频修复结合点云条件的方式有效解决了光照不一致和3D资产受限问题，实现了高质量、可扩展的自动驾驶仿真车辆插入。

Abstract: A pivotal step in autonomous driving simulation involves inserting foreground vehicles with predefined trajectories into simulated scenes. This process enhances scene diversity and facilitates the creation of various corner cases for testing and improving autonomous driving models. However, existing methods often rely on pre-reconstructed 3D assets, which frequently lead to lighting inconsistencies between the inserted foreground and the background. Moreover, the reliance on limited, manually-curated 3D assets hinders large-scale deployment. To address these challenges, we propose DriveWeaver, a novel framework for controllable vehicle insertion in autonomous driving simulation. Specifically, for a masked target insertion area, DriveWeaver performs video inpainting conditioned on vehicle point clouds to generate high-quality, temporally consistent vehicles. This video-inpainting-based approach ensures seamless blending between the foreground and background, while the readily available point cloud conditions enable superior generalization. To support long-term generation, we further design a global-to-local hierarchical inpainting strategy, ensuring the consistent identity and appearance of the inserted vehicles. Meanwhile, we extract explicit 3D Gaussian representations of the inserted vehicles through an urban reconstruction pipeline to enable real-time rendering for autonomous driving simulation. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines in visual realism and geometric consistency, providing a robust tool for scalable autonomous driving scene augmentation.

</details>


### [133] [InstanceControl: Controllable Complex Image Generation without Instance Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31924)
*Xiaoyu Liu,Huan Wang,Fan Li,Zhixin Wang,Jiaqi Xu,Ming Liu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstanceControl是一种无需实例标注的多实例可控图像生成方法，通过VLM自动建立文本提示与视觉条件之间的实例级对应关系，并采用自适应掩码细化策略在生成过程中动态优化实例掩码，实现了优于现有方法的保真度和精确实例级控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控图像生成方法（如ControlNet）在处理多实例场景时常出现实例属性混淆，而近期依赖人工实例标注的方法又过于耗费人力，因此需要一种无需实例标注的多实例可控生成方法。

Method: 利用视觉语言模型（VLM）自动从文本提示中解析实例描述并基于视觉条件预测实例掩码，同时引入自适应掩码细化策略在生成过程中动态优化掩码质量。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保真度和精确实例级控制方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: InstanceControl能有效解决多实例场景下的属性混淆问题，无需人工标注即可实现精确的实例级控制。

Abstract: Controllable image generation methods, such as ControlNet, have demonstrated a remarkable capacity to introduce visual conditions(e.g., depth maps) to guide image generation. However, these methods often struggle with complex multi-instance scenes, frequently leading to attribute confusion among instances. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate this via manual instance labeling, such requirements are labor-intensive. In this paper, we propose InstanceControl, a novel multi-instance controllable generation method that eliminates the need for instance labeling. We identify the primary bottleneck in existing methods as the inability to accurately associate instance descriptions with their corresponding regions within visual conditions. To address this, we leverage the Vision-Language Model (VLM) to establish instance-level correspondences between text prompts and visual conditions. Specifically, the VLM automatically parses instance descriptions from the text prompts and simultaneously predicts instance masks based on the visual conditions. Furthermore, since the predicted masks may contain noise, we introduce an adaptive mask refinement strategy that dynamically refines these instance masks during the generation process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior fidelity and precise instance-level control.

</details>


### [134] [No Place to Hide: Benchmarking Video Hallucination with Background-Controlled Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31933)
*Haojian Huang,Harold Haodong Chen,Meng Luo,Junjia Du,Shanqing Xu,Ziheng Chen,Yanxiang Huang,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VidPair-Halluc是一个用于评估大型视频模型幻觉的新基准，通过PairFlow管道构建背景相似但前景语义不同的视频对，包含1K个视频对和11K个时空QA对，覆盖10个语义方面，实验表明主流视频模型在对抗性设置中仍存在细粒度理解困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频幻觉基准主要依赖文本扰动或对抗性问题，忽略了视觉背景的一致性，导致无法准确将模型错误归因于真正的幻觉而非背景变化。

Method: 提出PairFlow管道，利用文本到图像和视频生成技术系统性地构建故事、生成连贯视频片段并组装成对抗性视频对；构建包含背景相似但前景语义不同的视频对，覆盖空间和时间推理共10个语义方面，生成1K高质量对抗视频对和11K时空QA对。

Result: 在主流大型视频模型上评估发现，模型在对抗性设置下仍存在鲁棒的细粒度视频理解困难。

Conclusion: VidPair-Halluc通过控制背景和前景变化，提供了更严格的视频幻觉评估基准，揭示了当前模型在对抗性场景中的局限性。

Abstract: We introduce VidPair-Halluc, a new benchmark for evaluating video hallucination in large video models (LVMs) under rigorous and controlled conditions. Unlike previous benchmarks that primarily rely on text-based perturbations or adversarial questions while neglecting the consistency of visual backgrounds, VidPair-Halluc features video pairs with highly similar backgrounds but distinctly different foreground semantics, enabling precise attribution of model errors to genuine hallucination rather than background variation. The benchmark is constructed through PairFlow, a pipeline that leverages recent advances in text-to-image and video generation to systematically compose stories, generate coherent video clips, and assemble them into adversarial pairs. Covering both spatial and temporal reasoning across ten semantic aspects, VidPair-Halluc comprises 1K high-quality adversarial video pairs and 11K spatio-temporal QA pairs with control over background and foreground variations. Evaluations on mainstream LVMs show persistent difficulty with robust fine-grained video understanding in adversarial settings, and code and data are available at the https://jethrojames.github.io/VidPair-Halluc/.

</details>


### [135] [World Narrative Model for Highly Controllable Video Generation: A Paradigm Shift from Pixel Sampling to Physical World Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31946)
*Ye Chen,Xuanhong Chen,Yupeng Zhu,Liming Tan,Zhewen Wan,Yuxuan Xiong,Tielong Wang,Jinfan Liu,Wuze Zhang,Xiongzhen Zhang,Feifei Li,Xianglin Luo,Zhehan Zhao,Zhifan Zhang,Laisheng Kou,Zhujing Liang,Yugang Chen,Muchun Chen,Xu Miao,Yijing Zhang,Xiaojie Sheng,Qiang Hu,Jialiang Chen,Weimin Zhang,Wenjun Zhang,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出世界叙事模型（WNM），通过将物理叙事（要渲染的内容）与像素生成（如何渲染）解耦，用协作代理将多模态输入转化为可编辑的4D世界表征（场景几何、物体布局、骨骼运动、轨迹、相机运动、光照等），作为确定性蓝图驱动视频基础模型生成最终视频，从而解决视频生成中缺乏可控性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型将视频视为像素分布采样问题，忽略了显式的实例级4D物理世界，导致内容创作者无法以确定性的量化方式指定几何、运动、相机参数和光照，陷入低效且昂贵的"gacha"循环，严重阻碍了工业级视频生成的发展。

Method: WNM采用解耦范式：1) 协作代理将稀疏多模态输入（文本、参考视频、草图）转化为结构化的4D世界表征（场景几何、物体布局、骨骼运动、轨迹、相机运动、光照）；2) 该确定性蓝图作为结构化的"神经着色器"，驱动冻结或轻适配的视频基础模型渲染最终画面；3) 构建人机协同平台，支持自动世界生成和预可视化，并提供导演控制台实现精细人工修正。

Result: 实验表明WNM大幅减少了概率性的"gacha"调用次数，生成的视频在布局、运动、电影摄影等方面紧密遵循创作者意图。

Conclusion: WNM是一个开放模块化的框架，各组件（世界表征、控制代理、适配器）可独立改进，为工业级可控视频生成提供了一种可行范式。

Abstract: The fundamental obstacle to industrial grade video generation is the lack of controllability: existing models treat video as a pixel distribution sampling problem, bypassing the explicit, instance level $4D$ $(3D + T)$ physical world. Consequently, content creators cannot specify geometry, motion, camera parameters, or lighting in a deterministic, quantitative way, leading to the infamous ''gacha'' loop that makes professional content creation prohibitively inefficient and expensive. To address this, we introduce the World Narrative Model (WNM), a paradigm that decouples what to render -- the structured physical narrative -- from how to render -- the pixel generation process. WNM replaces end-to-end black-box sampling with orchestrated $4D$ pre-visualization for media generation. Collaborative agents translate sparse multimodal inputs, including text, reference videos, and sketches, into a fully editable world representation with scene geometry, object layouts, character/animal skeleton motion, trajectories, camera motion, and lighting at quantitative, physically meaningful granularity. This representation acts as a deterministic structural blueprint that drives existing video foundation models, either frozen or lightly adapted, to render final footage, turning the base model into a faithful neural shader. Built on this engine, our human-AI platform supports automatic world generation and pre-visualization aligned with professional filmmaking pipelines, while director consoles enable seamless human refinement. Experiments show that WNM greatly reduces probabilistic ``gacha'' calls and produces videos whose layout, motion, and cinematography closely follow creator intent. The framework is open and modular, allowing each component, such as world representation, control agents, and adapters, to be independently improved. Project website: https://glassroom.sjtu.edu.cn/WNM/.

</details>


### [136] [AnyBokeh: Physics-Guided Any-to-Any Bokeh Editing with Optical Fingerprint Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31959)
*Xinyu Hou,Xiaoming Li,Zongsheng Yue,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyBokeh提出了一种物理引导的任意到任意散景编辑框架，通过估计源图像的符号弥散圆图和视差图，利用线性关系建模源光学指纹，实现了无需先恢复全聚焦图像即可完成的散景编辑，避免了重建伪影传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有散景编辑方法通常假设输入为全聚焦图像，或先恢复全聚焦图像再渲染新散景，这种管线会丢弃源图像中的模糊线索并将重建伪影传播到最终编辑结果中，无法在任意光圈和焦点设置下实现后捕获散景编辑。

Method: 1) 估计源图像的符号弥散圆(CoC)图和视差图；2) 建模符号弥散圆与视差差值的线性关系，估计源特定的光学指纹并传递到目标焦点和光圈设置；3) 基于源和目标CoC图的条件生成编辑器进行相对模糊合成，实现空间自适应的去模糊、保留和散景渲染；4) 构建高保真合成数据集用于物理监督学习。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，AnyBokeh在任意到任意散景编辑、全聚焦到散景渲染和散焦去模糊任务中均实现了忠实且可控的编辑效果，避免了现有方法所需的全聚焦重建和测试时散景级校准。

Conclusion: AnyBokeh通过物理引导的框架有效解决了单图像后捕获散景编辑难题，验证了保留和利用源图像模糊线索相比先重建全聚焦图像再编辑的优越性。

Abstract: Depth-of-field control is a fundamental tool in photography, yet post-capture bokeh editing from a single image remains challenging. A practical editor should handle images captured under arbitrary focus and aperture settings. Existing methods typically assume an all-in-focus input, or first recover an all-in-focus image before rendering new bokeh. Such pipelines can discard useful blur cues from the source image and propagate reconstruction artifacts into the final edit. We introduce AnyBokeh, a physics-guided framework for any-to-any bokeh editing. Instead of treating source blur merely as a degradation to be removed, AnyBokeh estimates the source blur state with a signed circle-of-confusion map and a disparity map. By modeling the linear relation between signed circle of confusion and disparity difference, AnyBokeh estimates a source-specific optical fingerprint and transfers the source optical characteristics to the desired focus and aperture setting. A generative editor conditioned on both source and target circle-of-confusion maps then performs relative blur synthesis, enabling spatially adaptive deblurring, preservation, and defocus rendering. To support physically supervised learning, we further construct a high-fidelity synthetic dataset with accurate depth, focus distance, and full EXIF metadata. Experiments on real-world benchmarks show that AnyBokeh achieves faithful and controllable editing across any-to-any bokeh editing, all-in-focus-to-bokeh rendering, and defocus deblurring, while avoiding all-in-focus reconstruction and test-time bokeh-level calibration commonly required by existing approaches. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/itsmag11/AnyBokeh.

</details>


### [137] [Planar-SfM: Camera Pose Estimation via Homography Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31979)
*Gabi Pragier,Matan Karklinsky,David Ungarish,Avi Ben-Cohen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的框架，将平面场景中的单应性分解作为几何约束，通过图结构和谱嵌入聚合多平面位姿估计，实现鲁棒的相机位姿恢复，在平面场景（如体育馆）和通用3D场景中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SfM方法在平面场景中因极线几何退化而失效，本文旨在将平面表面从限制转化为有利的几何约束来源，解决平面场景下的位姿估计难题。

Method: 利用多视图中的每个平面提供独立位姿估计（通过单应性分解），构建位姿图，采用谱嵌入识别并过滤不可靠边，提取最大一致性生成树用于位姿恢复。

Result: 在篮球场等高度平面场景中显著优于现有方法；在IMC Phototourism非约束户外场景基准上达到或超越最先进水平。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于单应性分解和图谱嵌入的SfM框架能自然处理从高度平面到通用3D环境的各种场景，证明平面约束并非缺陷而是可有效利用的几何信息源。

Abstract: Structure from Motion (SfM) systems traditionally struggle with planar scenes, where standard epipolar geometry-based methods become degenerate. Rather than viewing planar surfaces as a limitation, we propose a unified framework that leverages them as a source of geometric constraints. Our key insight is that each planar surface visible across multiple views provides an independent estimate of relative camera poses through homography decomposition. By aggregating estimates from multiple planes or even from a single dominant plane we achieve robust pose recovery in scenarios where traditional methods fail. We introduce a novel graph-based approach that constructs a pose-graph from homography estimates and employs spectral embedding to identify and filter unreliable edges. Our method maps homography-based pose estimates onto the real line based on their geometric and visual consistency, enabling efficient extraction of a maximally consistent spanning tree for pose recovery. This approach naturally handles both highly planar scenes, such as indoor sports arenas, and general $3$D environments. We demonstrate superior performance on basketball court imagery where existing methods struggle, while matching or exceeding state-of-the-art results on unconstrained outdoor scenes from the IMC Phototourism benchmark.

</details>


### [138] [ERA: Entropy-Guided Visual Token Pruning with Rectified Attention for Efficient MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31982)
*Yuhao Wang,Mu Qiao,Haiwen Diao,Yunzhi Zhuge,Pingping Zhang,Xindong Zhang,Lei Zhang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ERA框架，通过熵引导的视觉token剪枝和注意力对数校正，解决多模态大语言模型中因token减少导致的注意力对数崩溃问题，实现高效推理而不损害性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型因长视觉token序列导致推理成本高昂。现有训练无关的视觉token剪枝方法会扭曲注意力分布，引发注意力对数崩溃（Attention Logit Collapse）现象，亟需一种既能压缩token又能保持注意力分布完整性的方法。

Method: ERA包含三个核心组件：1) 双视角熵剪枝（DEP），通过联合建模视觉多样性和头级显著性识别代表性锚点token；2) 偏差感知token回收（BTR），将剪枝token回收到对应锚点并估计聚类级对数偏差；3) 对数保持注意力校正（LAR），将估计偏差注入注意力对数以校正token减少引起的崩溃。

Result: 在单图像、多图像和视频设置中，ERA在多种MLLM上实现了鲁棒的性能，即使在激进压缩下也能保留视觉证据，同时提供实际加速效果。

Conclusion: ERA建立了对数保持视觉token剪枝作为高效MLLM的规范化框架，统一了理论基础、算法设计和实际部署，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) incur prohibitive inference costs due to long visual token sequences. Training-free visual token reduction provides an efficient solution. However, existing methods distort attention distributions, giving rise to a phenomenon we term Attention Logit Collapse. To address this issue, we propose ERA, an Entropy-guided visual token pruning framework with Rectified Attention for efficient MLLMs. Specifically, ERA comprises three crucial components: Dual-view Entropy Pruning (DEP), Bias-aware Token Recycling (BTR), and Logit-preserving Attention Rectification (LAR). First, DEP identifies representative anchor tokens by jointly modeling visual diversity and head-wise saliency. BTR then recycles pruned tokens into their corresponding anchors while estimating a cluster-level logit bias. Building upon this, LAR injects the estimated bias into attention logits, effectively rectifying the collapse induced by token reduction. Together, these components preserve visual evidence even under aggressive compression, enabling robust performance across single-image, multi-image, and video settings on a wide range of MLLMs. Beyond delivering practical acceleration, ERA establishes logit-preserving visual token pruning as a principled framework for efficient MLLMs, unifying theoretical foundation, algorithmic design, and practical deployment. The code is at https://github.com/924973292/ERA.

</details>


### [139] [CoLT: Teaching Multi-Modal Models to Think with Chain of Latent Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31986)
*Lianyu Hu,Shengqian Qin,Zeqin Liao,Qing Guo,Liang Wan,Wei Feng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoLT提出了一种隐式链式推理框架，通过少量（最少3步）的潜在思考状态替代冗长的文本思维链，配合前向和后向解码器的步骤级监督以及内部一致性约束，在保持推理效率的同时超越现有潜在推理方法，实现10.1倍的推理加速和22.6倍的文本解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的思维链推理虽然提升了多模态大模型的复杂视觉推理能力，但其推理速度慢（需生成数千tokens）、受限于自然语言表达能力，亟需更高效且表达力更强的隐式推理范式。

Method: CoLT的核心方法包括：(1) 将推理过程压缩为少量（≥3步）的潜在思维表示；(2) 引入轻量级外部解码器，通过前向模式（将潜在状态解码为下一步文本推理）和后向模式（对齐解码器隐状态与给定前文的潜在思维）提供步骤级监督；(3) 内部监督机制确保潜在步骤间的连贯过渡；(4) 推理时移除解码器和内部监督以保持高效。

Result: 在8个基准上，CoLT优于CODI、SIM-CoT等现有潜在推理方法，以及依赖辅助图像标注的潜在视觉推理方法；相比文本CoT，推理时间减少10.1倍，文本解码时间减少22.6倍。

Conclusion: CoLT验证了通过少量潜在思维步骤进行高效隐式推理的可行性，在显著加速推理的同时不牺牲性能，为多模态模型推理提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has enabled multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) to tackle complex visual reasoning tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, this text-based reasoning paradigm is inherently slow at inference time with even thousands of tokens and fundamentally constrained by the expressiveness of natural language. In this paper, we propose CoLT, (Chain of Latent Thoughts), a novel framework that teaches multi-modal models to reason through a chain of latent thought representations instead of verbose text tokens, which can perform thinking with as few as 3 steps. Naively forcing the model to think with latent states easily produces meaningless semantics and makes training unstable. To effectively regulate the latent reasoning process, we introduce a lightweight external decoder that provides step-level supervision for each latent reasoning step in two complementary directions: a forward mode that decodes latent thoughts into the textual reasoning of the next step, and a backward mode that aligns decoder hidden states with the model's latent thoughts given preceding textual context. We further incorporate internal supervision that encourages coherent step-by-step latent transitions. The decoder and internal supervision are removed during inference to maintain high efficiency of latent reasoning. Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that CoLT not only outperforms existing latent reasoning methods such as CODI and SIM-CoT, but also surpasses latent visual reasoning approaches that rely on auxiliary images with costly annotation requirements. Compared to text CoT methods, CoLT can notably reduce the inference time by 10.1$\times$ and text decoding time by 22.6$\times$. Code is released at https://github.com/hulianyuyy/CoLT.

</details>


### [140] [Automated Background Swapping for Robustness against Spurious Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32018)
*Cesar Roder,Kajetan Schweighofer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoBackSwap方法，通过自动交换图像前后景来减少深度神经网络分类器对虚假背景特征的依赖，仅需少量patch标注即可训练辅助网络实现数据增强，在多种图像分类任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络容易依赖训练数据中的虚假相关性（如背景特征）进行分类，导致在测试分布偏移时性能严重下降，需要一种有效方法来减少分类器对这类虚假背景的依赖。

Method: 使用辅助网络将图像前景与背景分离，通过填充技术合成完整背景，然后将不同前景与修复后的背景组合以增强训练数据。仅需少量样本（几百张）的patch级标注即可训练辅助网络，自动增强整个训练集。

Result: 在多个具有虚假背景特征的图像分类任务中，AutoBackSwap一致优于先前方法。即使训练数据中没有打破虚假相关性的样本，该方法仍然非常有效。

Conclusion: AutoBackSwap通过自动前后景交换增强训练数据，有效降低了分类器对虚假背景的依赖，且所需标注成本低，在分布外泛化场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Classifiers based on Deep Neural Networks exhibit strong performance across domains, yet can fail catastrophically if they rely on spurious correlations, i.e., features that are predictive of the target label in the training data but are not causally linked and thus fail to generalize. For the vision domain, many such spurious correlations manifest themselves within the background of the image, where only the foreground is predictive of the class label. In this paper, we introduce Automated Background Swapping (AutoBackSwap) to reduce the reliance of classifiers on such spurious backgrounds. AutoBackSwap uses a secondary network to disentangle the foreground and background, followed by infilling to synthesize complete backgrounds, and finally combines different foregrounds and inpainted backgrounds to augment the training data. We find that patch-wise labeling of just a few hundred samples suffices to train the secondary network and automatically augment the full training dataset on challenging image classification tasks. In contrast to many previous methods, AutoBackSwap proves very effective even if there is not a single sample in the training data breaking the spurious correlation. Across a range of image classification tasks with spurious backgrounds, AutoBackSwap consistently outperforms prior methods.

</details>


### [141] [Cross-Space Distillation: Teaching One-Step Students with Modern Diffusion Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32020)
*Anh Nguyen,Ngan Nguyen,Duc Vu,Trung Dao,Viet Nguyen,Quan Dao,Kien Nguyen,Chi Tran,Phong Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen,Cuong Pham,Dimitris Metaxas,Vishal M. Patel,Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-Space Distillation框架，通过轻量级Bridge模块（冻结学生VAE解码器+可学习投影器）解决教师模型与学生模型因潜空间分辨率与VAE参数化不同而无法蒸馏的问题，成功将高性能大模型（如SD 3.5、Flux）的知识迁移至紧凑学生模型（如SD 1.5），在保持单步推理与低延迟的同时显著提升生成质量（HPSv3从5.4提升至9.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单步扩散模型的分布级时间步蒸馏方法假设教师与学生必须共享同一潜空间（Shared-Space constraint），导致高容量教师模型（如SD 3.5、Flux）无法将知识迁移至潜分辨率与VAE参数化不同的紧凑学生模型（如SD 1.5），限制了高效可部署模型的发展。

Method: 提出Bridge——一种轻量级潜空间接口，由冻结的学生VAE解码器（作为空间先验）与可学习的紧凑投影器组成。通过潜重建损失与注意力保真度损失进行训练，实现学生潜向量到教师空间的稳定对齐，无需修改学生主干网络。

Result: 在多种现代教师模型上，Bridge显著提升了紧凑单步学生模型的效果：SD 1.5的HPSv3从5.4提升至9.4，同时保持单步推理、低延迟与广泛的生态系统兼容性。

Conclusion: 异构大模型可通过轻量级潜空间接口蒸馏至高效可部署的骨干网络，证明了Cross-Space Distillation范式的有效性。

Abstract: Modern one-step diffusion models achieve impressive quality through distribution-based timestep distillation. Yet, they rely on a critical assumption: Teacher and Student must inhabit the same latent space. This Shared-Space constraint prevents knowledge transfer from modern high-capacity Teachers (e.g., SD 3.5 and Flux) into compact, deployment-friendly Students such as SD 1.5, whose latent resolution and VAE parameterization differ from the Teacher. We formalize this overlooked regime as Cross-Space Distillation, where Teacher and Student differ in both latent resolution and VAE space. To enable distillation under this mismatch, we introduce the Bridge, a lightweight latent interface that maps Student latents into the Teacher space without modifying the Student backbone. Bridge combines a frozen Student VAE decoder as a spatial prior with a compact learnable projector, and is trained with latent reconstruction and attention fidelity objectives for stable Teacher-space alignment. Across diverse modern Teachers, Bridge enables substantial gains for compact one-step Students; for example, it improves SD 1.5 from 5.4 to 9.4 HPSv3 while preserving one-step inference, low latency, and broad ecosystem compatibility. These results show that heterogeneous large Teachers can be distilled into efficient, deployable backbones through a lightweight latent-space interface.

</details>


### [142] [SpheRoPE: Zero-Shot Optimization-Free 360 Panorama Generation with Spherical RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32033)
*Or Hirschorn,Aaron Olender,Eli Alshan,Ianir Ideses,Lior Fritz,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpheRoPE框架，通过将球形先验注入预训练扩散Transformer，实现零样本、无需训练或优化的360°全景图像和视频生成。核心是用球形RoPE替代标准位置编码，并结合语义失真CFG，在无需微调的情况下解决等矩形投影的拓扑约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有360°全景生成方法要么依赖稀缺全景数据上的昂贵微调导致泛化受限，要么采用多步优化带来难以承受的推理延迟。作者发现预训练模型虽天然具备一定全景先验，但不足以满足ERP严格的拓扑约束，因此需要一种无需训练和优化的解决方案。

Method: 1) 球形RoPE：将低频通道重新参数化为3D笛卡尔坐标以编码球面流形，高频通道进行谐波量化以强制执行精确周期性；2) 语义失真CFG：显式引导几何结构，避免重训练并继承预训练模型的创作广度。

Result: 在Flux.1、Flux.2和LTX-Video等多种骨干网络上进行文本到全景生成，实现了与基线方法相竞争的性能，且完全无需训练。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种零样本、无需训练和优化的通用框架，能跨不同骨干网络和360°生成模态泛化，在保持创作多样性的同时有效解决了ERP的拓扑约束问题。

Abstract: We present a zero-shot, training-free and optimization-free framework for generating 360 panoramic images and videos by directly injecting spherical priors into pre-trained diffusion transformers. Existing methods either rely on costly fine-tuning on scarce panoramic data that limits generalization, or leverage multi-step optimization that incurs prohibitive inference latency. We observe that contemporary generative models natively exhibit some panoramic priors from large-scale training. However, these emergent capabilities are insufficient, as the models fundamentally fail to satisfy the rigorous topological constraints imposed by equirectangular projection (ERP). We introduce a zero-shot and optimization-free approach that resolves these constraints at inference time. Spherical RoPE replaces standard rotary position embeddings: low-frequency channels are re-parameterized as 3D Cartesian coordinates to natively encode the spherical manifold, while high-frequency channels are harmonically quantized to enforce exact periodicity. Coupled with complementary Semantic Distortion classifier-free guidance (CFG) that explicitly steers geometry, we avoid retraining and inherit the full creative breadth of state-of-the-art models. Our approach generalizes across diverse backbones and 360 generation modalities. We demonstrate this across text-to-panorama using Flux.1, Flux.2, and LTX-Video backbones, achieving competitive performance against baselines, all while remaining training-free. Project page: https://orhir.github.io/SpheRoPE

</details>


### [143] [PointSplat: Compact Gaussian Splatting via Human-Centric Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32036)
